## ===== briefs/ch34-t1-item8.md =====

# 12.2c tranche T1 — Item 8 (CH34-D2): the format-parameterized machine-code parse layer (new `CodeFormat.lean` + `CodeParser` + `MathlibBridge` + `NDCodes` + `Codes2Tape`)

## Repository and branch — read this before anything else

- Clone `https://github.com/Shilun-Allan-Li/tcslib` and check out branch
  **`complexity/arora-barak-ch3-4`**, this exact branch, NOT `main`.
- Confirm that `git merge-base --is-ancestor c1f9181cea266340e0e699a08d57ef414fd71a1c HEAD` succeeds. That
  commit archives the pre-ship probes cited below and the order list. If the
  check fails, **stop**.
- Create a working branch (suggested: `refactor/ch34-t1-codeformat`). Record the
  base commit hash in `REPORT.md`. Never rebase.
- **Delivery is by zip, not PR or push**: `ch34-t1-codeformat.zip` with `REPORT.md`,
  the full modified sources (new file included), a `git format-patch` series
  against your base, a bundle, the sweep, axiom and freeze logs, both copy-text
  screens, and `SHA256SUMS`.
- Integration (no action for you): side branch, a PR for the user's merge, and a
  separate audit gate.

## What this is

A 12.2c refactor discharging the **human-acknowledged debt CH34-D2**: `backlog.md`
§1, the Codes2Tape row of `audits/duplication-ledger.md`, and plan §4d, 12.2c
item 8.

After ZF-B2, **23 privates of `Codes2Tape.lean` are two-tape adaptations of the
one-tape top layer**: 20 of `CodeParser.lean` and 3 of `MathlibBridge.lean`. They
cover the record reader, table, parse, decode, scan and canonize, and their laws.
`NDCodes`' group-C fill would need a third copy.

You build the layer once, in three levels. **Level T** is a structure
`Turing.CodeFormat` over the *table* grammar (the formats differ in nesting depth,
and the ND table puts the choice bit outermost). **Level R** is a record reader
generic in the work-tape count `k`, via `codeReadVec` over `Action.workTapes`.
**Level W** is the shared 27-record two-work-tape table.

The one-tape and two-tape formats become instances. **The ND instance is not in
this batch**; it is `NDCodes`' group-C fill. **Zero `error:`, and no `sorry`
beyond the base's, after every commit.**

**Pre-ship checks** (maintainer, executed with `lean`, no .olean written; each 0
errors and 0 warnings):

- **`audits/evidence/ch34-t1/Item8PreShip.lean.txt`** imports `Codes2Tape`: every
  Level R/T/W declaration under its final name, both instances, the generic primrec
  layer and the transports D7a–D11; all 18 `#print axioms` within the triple.
- **`audits/evidence/ch34-t1/Item8MovePreShip.lean.txt`** imports only `CodeParser`
  and `Nondeterministic`: `workPair`/`actionBits₂` moved verbatim, the bridge,
  `NDCodes`' remainder, `Code2TM.serialize` and the 354-bit regression.
- **`audits/evidence/ch34-t1/Item8ContextPreShip.lean.txt`**: the `CodeFormat.lean`
  content under its single import, `CodeParser`.

**The main probe is the statement contract.** Every declaration it defines under
a final name must land with exactly that signature and statement. Its proofs are
working references; you may restructure them. The probe-only names are
`probeDecode`, `probeDecode2`, `probeFallback2`,
`probeFallback2_serialize_length`, `codePrimSkipAction'`, `probe_*` and the
`example`s. Read `policy.md` and `workflow.md` §4.

## Owned files (lines at the issue commit)

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| **new** `TuringMachine/CodeFormat.lean` | Import `TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.CodeParser` **only**. Contents: Levels R, T and W; the moved `workPair` and `actionBits₂`; the one-tape instance; the 5 moved one-tape publics. At most 600 lines. |
| `TuringMachine/CodeParser.lean` | Delete 16 privates; move 5 publics out. |
| `TuringMachine/MathlibBridge.lean` | Swap the import `CodeParser` → `CodeFormat`; add the generic primrec layer; delete 3 privates; turn `codeCanonical_machine` into a wrapper. |
| `TuringMachine/NDCodes.lean` | **Sanctioned move**: `workPair` (l. 98–101) and `actionBits₂` (l. 103–110) leave verbatim; add `import …CodeFormat`. Nothing else changes. |
| `TuringMachine/Codes2Tape.lean` | Delete 21 privates; add the private `twoTapeFormat`; retarget ZF-B3; change one public proof. |
| `TuringMachine.lean` (facade) | Add `import …CodeFormat` and one `## Contents` bullet. |

## Design (binding; the statements are in the main probe)

**Level R**, in `CodeFormat`, public: the definitions `actionBitsK`,
`codeReadWork`, `codeReadActionK`, `codeSkipWork`, `codeSkipActionK`; the laws
`codeReadWork_append`, `codeReadActionK_append`, `codeReadWork_sound`,
`codeReadActionK_sound`, `codeEraseWork`, `codeEraseActionK` and
`codeActionK_length : 4 * k + 5 ≤ (actionBitsK a).length`; the moved `workPair`
and `actionBits₂` (byte-identical, docstrings included); and the bridges
`actionBits_eq_actionBitsK`, `actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK` and
`codeSkipAction_eq_codeSkipActionK : codeSkipAction n xs = codeSkipActionK 1 n xs`.
Private: `codeReadActionK_one_append`, `codeReadActionK_one_sound`,
`codeEraseActionK_one`.

**Level T**, in `CodeFormat`, public. The structure, as probed:

```lean
structure CodeFormat where
  Code : Type
  Table : ℕ → Type
  numStates : Code → ℕ
  assemble : (n : ℕ) → Fin (n + 1) → Table n → Code
  serialize : Code → List Bool
  tableBits : {n : ℕ} → Table n → List Bool
  readTable : (n : ℕ) → List Bool → Option (Table n × List Bool)
  skipTable : ℕ → List Bool → Option (List Bool)
  guard : ℕ
  fallback : Code
  numStates_assemble : ∀ n q t, numStates (assemble n q t) = n
  serialize_assemble : ∀ n q (t : Table n),
    serialize (assemble n q t) = pairEncode n.bits (unaryFin q ++ tableBits t)
  assemble_surj : ∀ M, ∃ n q t, assemble n q t = M
  readTable_append : ∀ n (t : Table n) xs, readTable n (tableBits t ++ xs) = some (t, xs)
  readTable_sound : ∀ n xs (t : Table n) rest,
    readTable n xs = some (t, rest) → xs = tableBits t ++ rest
  readTable_erase : ∀ n xs, (readTable n xs).map Prod.snd = skipTable n xs
  tableBits_length : ∀ n (t : Table n), guard * (n + 1) ≤ (tableBits t).length
```

Definitions: `CodeFormat.parseFull`, `scan`, `canonical`, and
`decode := ((F.parseFull xs).map Prod.fst).getD F.fallback` (so no `parse_full`).
Theorems: `parseFull_serialize_pad`, `decode_serialize_pad`, `parseFull_sound`,
`scan_full`, `canonical_eq`. B5-facing corollaries: `serialize_decode_length_le`,
`numStates_decode_le`, `decode_of_parseFull_none`, `decode_of_guard_lt`.

**Level W**, in `CodeFormat`, public: `CodeTable2` (an `abbrev`),
`codeTable2Bits`, `codeReadTable2`, `codeSkipTable2`, and the laws
`codeReadTable2_append`, `codeReadTable2_sound`, `codeEraseTable2`,
`codeTable2Bits_length` (351).

**`oneTapeFormat`**, in `CodeFormat`, public, fields exactly as probed:
`serialize := CodeTM.serialize`; `tableBits` is the nested flatMap over the
frozen `actionBits`, so `serialize_assemble` is `rfl`; `readTable` uses
`codeReadActionK 1 n`; `skipTable` uses **the frozen public `codeSkipAction`**,
which keeps the new `codeScan` definitionally the old one; `guard := 81`;
`fallback := codeFallback`.

**MathlibBridge.** Public: `codePrimSkipWork`, `codePrimSkipActionK`,
`CodeFormat.primrec_scan`, `CodeFormat.primrec_canonical`,
`CodeFormat.exists_canonizer`, `codePrimSkipTable2`. Private:
`codePrimSkipTable1 : Primrec₂ oneTapeFormat.skipTable`, built from
`codePrimSkipActionK 1` and the `codeSkipAction` bridge.

**Codes2Tape**, private: `twoTapeFormat`, with fields as probed except
`fallback := zfBFallback`; declare it after `zfBFallback`.

No new lemma carries `@[simp]`. Every public declaration gets a docstring with
`[AB09, §1.4]` attribution. `parseFull_serialize_pad`, `parseFull_sound`,
`scan_full` and `codeTable2Bits_length` each carry a "Proof sketch", adapted
from the deleted originals'.

## Tasks

**Task 1 — the layer and the one-tape side** (CodeFormat, CodeParser, NDCodes,
MathlibBridge, facade).

1. Create `CodeFormat.lean`. Order: a header with `CodeParser`'s `set_option`s;
   Level R; the moved definitions; the bridges; Level T; Level W; the private
   corollaries; `oneTapeFormat`; the moved one-tape publics.
2. In NDCodes, delete the two moved definitions and add the import.
3. In CodeParser, delete the 16 privates and the 5 moved publics.
4. In MathlibBridge, put the generic layer and `codePrimSkipTable1/2` where
   `codePrimCanonical` stood (after `codePrimPrefix`, before `BridgeAlphabet`).
   Delete `codePrimSkipAction` (l. 178), `codePrimScan` (230) and
   `codePrimCanonical` (269). Re-prove the private `codeCanonical_machine` (1078)
   as `oneTapeFormat.exists_canonizer codePrimSkipTable1` (D10). Its statement is
   unchanged, so `exists_effectiveMachineCode` stays **byte-identical**.
5. **Checkpoint**: replay, freeze, axioms. Codes2Tape must still elaborate
   unchanged, reaching the moved definitions through NDCodes → CodeFormat. A
   Task-1-complete partial delivery is acceptable.

**Task 2 — the two-tape side** (Codes2Tape).

1. Add `twoTapeFormat`. Keep `zfBDecode` as the typed alias
   `private def zfBDecode (xs : List Bool) : Code2TM := twoTapeFormat.decode xs`,
   so every ZF-B3 statement that mentions it stays byte-identical.
2. Delete the 21 privates.
3. Re-prove `zfB3_decode_length`, `zfB3_decode_states` and `zfB3_guard_failure`
   by instantiation, statements unchanged
   (`change … at h; rwa [zfBFallback_serialize_length] at h`, D9). In
   `zfB3Answer_malformed` the hypothesis becomes
   `(hparse : twoTapeFormat.parseFull α = none)` and `hd` becomes
   `twoTapeFormat.decode_of_parseFull_none α hparse`.
4. Re-prove `zfB3_uniform_of_simulator` and `exists_effectiveMachineCode2` via
   `twoTapeFormat.exists_canonizer codePrimSkipTable2`, with
   `decode_encode_pad := twoTapeFormat.decode_serialize_pad` (D11). Every other
   ZF-B3 private stays byte-identical.

**Priority on exhaustion:** Task 1, then Task 2; partial means Task 2 unstarted,
never an admission.

## Ground rules (binding)

1. **Ownership**: exactly the six files above.
2. **Statement freeze.** Every public declaration of the six files keeps its
   signature, statement, docstring and proof byte-identical, except:
   - **(a) Moved**, chunk otherwise unchanged: `workPair` and `actionBits₂`
     (NDCodes → CodeFormat); `codeDecode`, `codeDecode_serialize_pad`, `codeScan`,
     `codeCanonical` and `codeCanonical_eq` (CodeParser → CodeFormat).
   - **(b) Definition bodies:** `codeDecode`, `codeScan` and `codeCanonical`
     become `oneTapeFormat.decode/scan/canonical xs`.
   - **(c) Proof bodies:** `codeDecode_serialize_pad` and `codeCanonical_eq`
     become `oneTapeFormat.decode_serialize_pad M m` and
     `oneTapeFormat.canonical_eq xs`; `exists_effectiveMachineCode2` (Task 2).
   - **(d) Flagged, prefix-preserving "Item-8 appendix" paragraphs**, on
     `exists_effectiveMachineCode2`'s docstring (its ZF-B appendix stays) and on
     the module docstrings of CodeParser, MathlibBridge, NDCodes and Codes2Tape.
   - **(e)** Codes2Tape's section comment `/-! ### Private parser and canonizer
     implementation (ZF-B) … -/` (l. 151–162) may be rewritten. **(f)** The only
     private statement change is `zfB3Answer_malformed`'s hypothesis.

   Everything else stays byte-identical, in particular `codeFallback`,
   `codeSkipAction`, `exists_effectiveMachineCode`, and every NDCodes statement.
   The freeze comparison runs over the **union of the six files**, since names
   move between files.
3. **Transports are term-mode.** Derive per-format facts from generic ones by
   `exact`/application, or `change`/`show` first. `rw`/`simp` with a generic
   lemma on a per-format term matches syntactically and fails (probed:
   D7a/c/e, D9–D11).
4. **Deletions (exact).**
   - **CodeParser:** `codeReadAction`, `codeParse`, `codeReadAction_append`,
     `codeAction_length`, `codeTable_length`, `codeReadTable_append`,
     `codeParse_serialize_pad`, `codePairDecode_sound` (twins the public
     `eq_pairEncode_of_pairDecode`; cite that instead), `codeReadAction_sound`,
     `codeAllTrue_eq` and `codeParse_sound` (both dead), `codeEraseAction`,
     `codeParseFull`, `codeParse_full`, `codeScan_full`, `codeParseFull_sound`.
   - **MathlibBridge:** the three listed in Task 1.
   - **Codes2Tape:** `zfBReadAction`, `zfBParse`, `zfBReadAction_append`,
     `zfBAction_length`, `zfBTable_length`, `zfBReadTable_append`,
     `zfBParse_serialize_pad`, `zfBDecode_serialize_pad`, `zfBReadAction_sound`,
     `zfBSkipAction`, `zfBEraseAction`, `zfBParseFull`, `zfBScan`,
     `zfBParse_full`, `zfBScan_full`, `zfBParseFull_sound`, `zfBCanonical`,
     `zfBCanonical_eq`, `zfBPrimSkipAction`, `zfBPrimScan`, `zfBPrimCanonical`.
   - **Kept:** `zfBFallback`, `zfBFallback_serialize_length`, and `zfBDecode`
     (as the alias).
5. **Imports**: exactly the four changes in the table; Codes2Tape's imports are
   unchanged. No cycle can arise: CodeFormat's upstream is CodeParser's plus
   itself, and none of its importers is upstream of CodeParser.
6. **Counts** (public/private, base → target; justify any deviation): CodeParser
   46/16 → 41/0; CodeFormat → 45 (38 new + 7 moved)/3; MathlibBridge 15/62 →
   21/60; NDCodes 9/0 → 7/0; Codes2Tape 9/40 → 9/20.
7. **Escalation**: if a probed statement fails in situ, restore the original,
   record it, and continue. Never weaken a statement or add an admission.

- [ ] `freeze.log` over the six-file union: the moves; exactly the sanctioned
      bodies (2b–c) and appendices (2d–e); the private change (2f); every
      deletion and new declaration with its role; the counts against rule 6.
- [ ] Imports: the four changes only; probe statements verbatim, or justified.
- [ ] Duplication: both screens, the CH34-D2 pairs gone, every new pair
      classified, "new copies: none".
- [ ] The final sweep tail (23 modules, plus Codes2Tape checked directly), the
      123 axiom prints with the two audit programs, and the lint line.
- [ ] The diff touches only the six owned files.

## Known pitfalls at this pin (from the pre-ship iterations)

- **A structure field named `mk` clashes with the constructor `CodeFormat.mk`.**
  That is why the field is `assemble`.
- **`parseFull_sound`**: the `readTable_sound` term carries an unreduced
  `(q, r₁).2`. Do `have htable := F.readTable_sound _ _ _ _ ht; dsimp only at
  htable` before `rw`, as `zfBParseFull_sound` does.
- **`parseFull_serialize_pad`**: the final `simp only` needs no `bind`,
  `Option.bind` or `Bool.or_true`; the unused-argument linter flags them. `omega`
  treats `F.guard * (n + 1)` as an atom.
- **`show X from e` split across lines** inside a `by` nested in a term ends the
  tactic block. Use `have he : … := funext F.canonical_eq; rw [he] at hM` instead.
- **Write structure-instance literals one field per line**; a `{ a := …, b := …,`
  line continued at a smaller column fails to parse. **The 354-bit regression**
  needs the *named* fallback plus `dsimp only` before `norm_num`.
- **Dot notation on `F.Code`-typed terms resolves**, e.g.
  `(twoTapeFormat.decode α).toFinTM`. The aliases exist only to keep frozen text
  byte-identical.
- **`twoTapeFormat`**: `serialize_assemble` is
  `simp [Code2TM.serialize, codeTable2Bits, actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK, workPair]`;
  `assemble_surj` is `congr 1` twice, then `fin_cases i <;> simp [workPair]` (the
  one-tape analogue uses `Subsingleton.elim` on `Fin 1`).
- **Primrec**: `codeSkipActionK` has three binds, so its state argument sits under
  **four** `Primrec.fst`. `CodeFormat.primrec_scan` is `codePrimScan` with
  `Primrec.const F.guard` and the hypothesis in place of the nested
  `codePrimRepeat`. Deleting a declaration takes its docstring with it; moving
  one moves the docstring byte-identically.

## ===== audits/evidence/ch34-t1/Item8PreShip.lean.txt =====

/-
Item 8 (CH34-D2) pre-ship probe, revision 2.  ELABORATED with the maintainer's read-only
runner (`bash <S>/tools/leanprobe.sh`, no .olean output): 0 errors, 0 warnings, 0 sorries.
Every `#print axioms` below is within [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]; output in
`item8-probe.out`.  Revision 1 (`item8-probe.v1.lean`) failed on four syntax/locality slips:
an extra `⟩` in an `obtain` pattern; a `rw` blocked by an unreduced `(q, r₁).2`, fixed by
`dsimp only` on the soundness term first, as `zfBParseFull_sound` does; a `show … from`
line break inside a `by` nested in a term; and one-line structure-instance literals.
None of them was a design issue.

* Base: HEAD 5ad155ee58b83806b34b2a5c97da21363cda92ec (Lean v4.25.0, Mathlib 029db123ddaa7f8fd0d18cea3b1b33bf84dacd1e).
  It is equally valid at 44853c6f3177fed8f7f8d2288210593e9c094f7e. That delta leaves
  CodeParser, Codes2Tape, NDCodes, MathlibBridge and Encoding unchanged; upstream,
  `Finite.lean` gained one unrelated lemma.
* A scratch module.  It imports the HEAD tree only and edits nothing.  It defines the
  proposed layer under its proposed names.  All of them are fresh at HEAD: grep over
  `TCSlib/` and the pinned Mathlib found none of `CodeFormat`, `actionBitsK`,
  `codeReadWork`, `codeReadActionK`, `codeSkipWork`, `codeSkipActionK`, `CodeTable2`,
  `codeTable2Bits`, `oneTapeFormat`, `twoTapeFormat`.
* `probeDecode` stands in for the one-tape public `codeDecode`, which already exists at
  HEAD.  `codeScan` and `codeCanonical` are compared with the existing public
  definitions directly, by `rfl`.
* Expected: 0 errors, 0 sorries.  Every `#print axioms` should be within
  [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound].
* Design-critical checks are marked (D1)–(D8).  Proofs that are only mechanical
  adaptations of existing ones are marked (M).  If an (M) proof fails, it can be patched
  locally.  If a (D) check fails, the design needs revisiting before the brief.
-/
import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.Codes2Tape

set_option maxHeartbeats 0
set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
set_option autoImplicit false

namespace Turing

/-! ## Stage A: Level R, the transition record generic in the work-tape count `k` -/

/-- The fixed serialization of one record of a `k`-work-tape machine: the input move,
each work tape's optional write and move in tape order, the emission, the successor. -/
def actionBitsK {k n : ℕ} (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1))) : List Bool :=
  signBits a.inputTape ++
    (List.finRange k).flatMap
      (fun i => optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes i).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes i).2) ++
    optBoolBits a.output ++ optStateBits a.state

/-- Read one work-tape field pair: the optional write, then the move. -/
def codeReadWork (xs : List Bool) :
    Option ((Option (Option Bool) × SignType) × List Bool) := do
  let (wr, xs) ← codeReadWrite xs
  let (wm, xs) ← codeReadSign xs
  pure ((wr, wm), xs)

/-- Read one `k`-work-tape record. The work-tape actions are read as a
`Fin k`-indexed vector, so the record reconstructs `Action.workTapes` exactly. -/
def codeReadActionK (k n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
    Option (Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1)) × List Bool) := do
  let (im, xs) ← codeReadSign xs
  let (ws, xs) ← codeReadVec codeReadWork k xs
  let (out, xs) ← codeReadOutput xs
  let (q, xs) ← codeReadState (n + 1) xs
  pure (⟨im, ws, out, q⟩, xs)

/-- The erased work-pair reader. -/
def codeSkipWork (xs : List Bool) : Option (List Bool) :=
  (codeSkipPair (fun _ _ => true) xs).bind (codeSkipPair (fun a b => a || !b))

/-- The erased `k`-work-tape record reader. -/
def codeSkipActionK (k n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) : Option (List Bool) := do
  let xs ← codeSkipPair (fun a b => a || !b) xs
  let xs ← codeSkipRepeat codeSkipWork k xs
  let xs ← codeSkipPair (fun a b => a || !b) xs
  codeSkipState (n + 1) xs

-- (M)
lemma codeReadWork_append (p : Option (Option Bool) × SignType) (xs : List Bool) :
    codeReadWork ((optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2) ++ xs) = some (p, xs) := by
  rcases p with ⟨wr, wm⟩
  simp only [codeReadWork, List.append_assoc, codeReadWrite_append, codeReadSign_append,
    bind, Option.bind, pure]

-- (D1) the vector read reconstructs `workTapes` with no `Subsingleton`/`fin_cases` step
lemma codeReadActionK_append {k n : ℕ} (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1))) (xs : List Bool) :
    codeReadActionK k n (actionBitsK a ++ xs) = some (a, xs) := by
  rcases a with ⟨im, ws, out, q⟩
  have hv : ∀ ys, codeReadVec codeReadWork k
      ((List.finRange k).flatMap (fun i => optOptBoolBits (ws i).1 ++ signBits (ws i).2) ++ ys) =
        some (ws, ys) :=
    codeReadVec_append codeReadWork (fun p => optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2)
      codeReadWork_append k ws
  simp only [actionBitsK, List.append_assoc, codeReadActionK, codeReadSign_append, hv,
    codeReadOutput_append, codeReadState_append, bind, Option.bind, pure]

-- (M)
lemma codeReadWork_sound (xs : List Bool) (p : Option (Option Bool) × SignType)
    (rest : List Bool) (h : codeReadWork xs = some (p, rest)) :
    xs = (optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2) ++ rest := by
  simp only [codeReadWork, bind, Option.bind_eq_some_iff] at h
  obtain ⟨⟨wr, r₁⟩, h₁, ⟨⟨wm, r₂⟩, h₂, h⟩⟩ := h
  simp only [pure, Option.some.injEq, Prod.mk.injEq] at h
  rcases h with ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
  rw [codeReadWrite_sound xs wr r₁ h₁, codeReadSign_sound r₁ wm r₂ h₂]
  simp [List.append_assoc]

-- (M)
lemma codeReadActionK_sound {k n : ℕ} (xs : List Bool) (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1)))
    (rest : List Bool) (h : codeReadActionK k n xs = some (a, rest)) :
    xs = actionBitsK a ++ rest := by
  simp only [codeReadActionK, bind, Option.bind_eq_some_iff] at h
  obtain ⟨⟨im, r₁⟩, h₁, ⟨⟨ws, r₂⟩, h₂, ⟨⟨out, r₃⟩, h₃, ⟨⟨q, r₄⟩, h₄, h⟩⟩⟩⟩ := h
  simp only [pure, Option.some.injEq, Prod.mk.injEq] at h
  rcases h with ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
  rw [codeReadSign_sound xs im r₁ h₁,
    codeReadVec_sound codeReadWork (fun p => optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2)
      codeReadWork_sound k r₁ ws r₂ h₂,
    codeReadOutput_sound r₂ out r₃ h₃, codeReadState_sound r₃ q r₄ h₄]
  simp [actionBitsK, List.append_assoc]

-- (M) mirrors the last two steps of `codeEraseAction`
lemma codeEraseWork (xs : List Bool) : (codeReadWork xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipWork xs := by
  simp only [codeReadWork, codeSkipWork, bind, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def, pure,
    Option.map_some]
  rw [← codeEraseWrite xs, ← codeErase_bind]
  congr 1; funext p
  simpa only [Option.map_eq_bind, Function.comp_def] using codeEraseSign p.2

-- (M) mirrors `codeEraseAction`, with one vector step
lemma codeEraseActionK (k n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
    (codeReadActionK k n xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipActionK k n xs := by
  have hv (ys : List Bool) :
      (codeReadVec codeReadWork k ys).map Prod.snd = codeSkipRepeat codeSkipWork k ys := by
    rw [codeEraseVec]
    exact congrArg (fun r => codeSkipRepeat r k ys) (funext codeEraseWork)
  simp only [codeReadActionK, codeSkipActionK, bind, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def, pure,
    Option.map_some]
  rw [← codeEraseSign xs, ← codeErase_bind]
  congr 1; funext p
  rw [← hv p.2, ← codeErase_bind]
  congr 1; funext p
  rw [← codeEraseOutput p.2, ← codeErase_bind]
  congr 1; funext p
  simpa only [Option.map_eq_bind, Function.comp_def] using codeEraseState (n + 1) p.2

-- (M) generalizes `codeAction_length` (9 = 4·1 + 5) and `zfBAction_length` (13 = 4·2 + 5)
lemma codeActionK_length {k n : ℕ} (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1))) :
    4 * k + 5 ≤ (actionBitsK a).length := by
  have hs (s : SignType) : (signBits s).length = 2 := by cases s <;> rfl
  have ho (b : Option Bool) : (optBoolBits b).length = 2 := by
    rcases b with _ | b
    · rfl
    · cases b <;> rfl
  have hw (b : Option (Option Bool)) : (optOptBoolBits b).length = 2 := by
    rcases b with _ | (_ | b)
    · rfl
    · rfl
    · cases b <;> rfl
  have hq : 1 ≤ (optStateBits a.state).length := by
    cases a.state <;> simp [optStateBits, unaryFin]
  have hv : 4 * k ≤ ((List.finRange k).flatMap fun i =>
      optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes i).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes i).2).length := by
    simpa using codeFlatMap_length (List.finRange k)
      (fun i => optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes i).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes i).2) 4
      (fun i _ => by simp [hs, hw])
  simp only [actionBitsK, List.length_append, hs, ho]
  omega

-- (D2) the frozen one-tape record is the `k = 1` record (propositionally)
lemma actionBits_eq_actionBitsK {n : ℕ} (a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) :
    actionBits a = actionBitsK a := by
  simp [actionBits, actionBitsK, List.finRange_succ, List.append_assoc]

-- (D2) the frozen two-tape record is the `k = 2` record (propositionally).  In the real
-- change it lives in Codes2Tape (private), because `actionBits₂` is NDCodes-owned.
lemma actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK {n : ℕ} (a : Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) :
    actionBits₂ a = actionBitsK a := by
  simp [actionBits₂, actionBitsK, List.finRange_succ, List.append_assoc]

-- (D3) the frozen public one-tape skipper agrees with the generic one; this keeps
-- `codeSkipAction`'s body (and `MathlibBridge`'s `unfold codeSkipAction`) untouched
lemma codeSkipAction_eq_codeSkipActionK (n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
    codeSkipAction n xs = codeSkipActionK 1 n xs := by
  have h1 (ys : List Bool) : codeSkipRepeat codeSkipWork 1 ys = codeSkipWork ys := by
    cases h : codeSkipWork ys <;> simp [codeSkipRepeat, h]
  simp only [codeSkipAction, codeSkipActionK, h1, codeSkipWork, bind, Option.bind_assoc]

/-! ## Stage B: Level T, the format-parameterized top layer -/

/-- A machine-code format over the shared header grammar
`pairEncode (Nat.bits n) (unaryFin q₀ ++ table)`: the coded machines, their tables, the
table grammar (writer, reader, erased reader, per-state minimum length) and the
fallback, with the laws that the generic parser proofs consume.
(The field is `assemble`, not `mk`: a field named `mk` would clash with the
structure's default constructor `CodeFormat.mk`.) -/
structure CodeFormat where
  /-- the coded machines -/
  Code : Type
  /-- the transition table of a machine with `n + 1` states -/
  Table : ℕ → Type
  /-- one less than the number of states -/
  numStates : Code → ℕ
  /-- assemble a machine from its state count, initial state and table -/
  assemble : (n : ℕ) → Fin (n + 1) → Table n → Code
  /-- the format's fixed serialization -/
  serialize : Code → List Bool
  /-- the fixed serialization of a table -/
  tableBits : {n : ℕ} → Table n → List Bool
  /-- the table reader -/
  readTable : (n : ℕ) → List Bool → Option (Table n × List Bool)
  /-- the erased table reader -/
  skipTable : ℕ → List Bool → Option (List Bool)
  /-- the minimum number of table bits per state (the up-front guard constant) -/
  guard : ℕ
  /-- the machine denoted by every malformed string -/
  fallback : Code
  numStates_assemble : ∀ n q t, numStates (assemble n q t) = n
  serialize_assemble : ∀ n q (t : Table n),
    serialize (assemble n q t) = pairEncode n.bits (unaryFin q ++ tableBits t)
  assemble_surj : ∀ M, ∃ n q t, assemble n q t = M
  readTable_append : ∀ n (t : Table n) xs, readTable n (tableBits t ++ xs) = some (t, xs)
  readTable_sound : ∀ n xs (t : Table n) rest,
    readTable n xs = some (t, rest) → xs = tableBits t ++ rest
  readTable_erase : ∀ n xs, (readTable n xs).map Prod.snd = skipTable n xs
  tableBits_length : ∀ n (t : Table n), guard * (n + 1) ≤ (tableBits t).length

namespace CodeFormat

variable (F : CodeFormat)

/-- Parse the header and the table, keeping the unconsumed padding. The length guard
precedes table recursion; the suffix must be all `true`. -/
def parseFull (xs : List Bool) : Option (F.Code × List Bool) := do
  let (bits, rest) ← pairDecode xs
  let n := codeBitsNat bits
  if bits ≠ n.bits then none else do
    if F.guard * (n + 1) > rest.length then none else do
      let (q, rest) ← codeReadFin (n + 1) rest
      let (table, rest) ← F.readTable n rest
      if rest.all id then pure (F.assemble n q table, rest) else none

/-- Total decoding: every malformed string denotes the fallback. -/
def decode (xs : List Bool) : F.Code := ((F.parseFull xs).map Prod.fst).getD F.fallback

/-- The erased parser: same guard and padding test, returning only the suffix. -/
def scan (xs : List Bool) : Option (List Bool) := do
  let (bits, rest) ← pairDecode xs
  let n := codeBitsNat bits
  if bits ≠ n.bits then none else do
    if F.guard * (n + 1) > rest.length then none else do
      let rest ← codeSkipFin (n + 1) rest
      let rest ← F.skipTable n rest
      if rest.all id then pure rest else none

/-- The consumed prefix on scanner success, the fallback's serialization otherwise. -/
def canonical (xs : List Bool) : List Bool :=
  (F.scan xs).casesOn (F.serialize F.fallback) fun tail => xs.take (xs.length - tail.length)

-- (D4) the generic round trip; `omega` must treat `F.guard * (n + 1)` as an atom
theorem parseFull_serialize_pad (M : F.Code) (m : ℕ) :
    F.parseFull (F.serialize M ++ List.replicate m true) =
      some (M, List.replicate m true) := by
  obtain ⟨n, q, t, rfl⟩ := F.assemble_surj M
  have hp (a b c : List Bool) : pairEncode a b ++ c = pairEncode a (b ++ c) := by
    simp [pairEncode, List.append_assoc]
  rw [F.serialize_assemble, hp]
  unfold parseFull
  rw [pairDecode_pairEncode]
  dsimp only [bind, Option.bind]
  rw [codeBitsNat_bits]
  simp only [ne_eq, not_true_eq_false, ↓reduceIte]
  have hlen := F.tableBits_length n t
  simp only [List.length_append, List.length_replicate] at *
  rw [if_neg (by omega)]
  simp only [List.append_assoc, codeReadFin_append, F.readTable_append,
    List.all_replicate, id_eq, ite_self, ↓reduceIte, pure]

theorem decode_serialize_pad (M : F.Code) (m : ℕ) :
    F.decode (F.serialize M ++ List.replicate m true) = M := by
  simp only [decode, parseFull_serialize_pad, Option.map_some, Option.getD_some]

-- (M) mirrors `zfBParseFull_sound`
theorem parseFull_sound (xs : List Bool) (M : F.Code) (tail : List Bool)
    (h : F.parseFull xs = some (M, tail)) : xs = F.serialize M ++ tail := by
  unfold parseFull at h
  obtain ⟨⟨bits, rest⟩, hp, h⟩ := Option.bind_eq_some_iff.mp h
  dsimp only at h
  split at h
  · contradiction
  next hb =>
    have hb : bits = (codeBitsNat bits).bits := not_not.mp hb
    split at h
    · contradiction
    next _ =>
      simp only [bind, Option.bind_eq_some_iff] at h
      obtain ⟨⟨q, r₁⟩, hq, ⟨⟨table, r₂⟩, ht, h⟩⟩ := h
      split at h
      next hpad =>
        simp only [pure, Option.some.injEq, Prod.mk.injEq] at h
        rcases h with ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
        have htable := F.readTable_sound _ _ _ _ ht
        dsimp only at htable
        rw [F.serialize_assemble, eq_pairEncode_of_pairDecode xs bits rest hp,
          codeReadFin_sound rest q r₁ hq, htable]
        simp only [pairEncode, List.append_assoc]
        congr 1
        exact congrArg (List.flatMap fun b : Bool => [b, b]) hb
      · contradiction

-- (M) mirrors `zfBScan_full`
theorem scan_full (xs : List Bool) : F.scan xs = (F.parseFull xs).map Prod.snd := by
  simp only [scan, parseFull, bind, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def]
  congr 1
  funext p
  dsimp only
  split <;> try simp only [Option.map_none]
  split <;> try simp only [Option.map_none, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def]
  have h (q : Fin (codeBitsNat p.1 + 1) × List Bool) (t : F.Table (codeBitsNat p.1) × List Bool) :
      (if t.2.all id then some (F.assemble (codeBitsNat p.1) q.1 t.1, t.2) else none).map
          Prod.snd = (if t.2.all id then some t.2 else none) := by split <;> rfl
  simp only [pure, h]
  rw [← codeEraseFin _ _, ← codeErase_bind]
  congr 1; funext q
  rw [← F.readTable_erase, ← codeErase_bind]

-- (M) mirrors `zfBCanonical_eq`; `parse_full` disappears because `decode` is defined
-- from `parseFull` directly
theorem canonical_eq (xs : List Bool) : F.canonical xs = F.serialize (F.decode xs) := by
  unfold canonical decode
  rw [scan_full]
  cases h : F.parseFull xs with
  | none => rfl
  | some p =>
    rcases p with ⟨M, tail⟩
    simp only [Option.map_some, Option.getD_some]
    rw [F.parseFull_sound xs M tail h]
    simp

-- (B5-facing corollaries; generalize ZF-B3's `zfB3_decode_length`/`_states`/`_guard_failure`)
theorem serialize_decode_length_le (α : List Bool) :
    (F.serialize (F.decode α)).length ≤ max α.length (F.serialize F.fallback).length := by
  rw [← F.canonical_eq]
  unfold canonical
  cases F.scan α with
  | none => exact Nat.le_max_right _ _
  | some tail => exact (List.length_take_le' _ _).trans (Nat.le_max_left _ _)

theorem numStates_decode_le (α : List Bool) :
    F.guard * (F.numStates (F.decode α) + 1) ≤
      max α.length (F.serialize F.fallback).length := by
  have hsize := F.serialize_decode_length_le α
  obtain ⟨n, q, t, h⟩ := F.assemble_surj (F.decode α)
  rw [← h, F.serialize_assemble, length_pairEncode, List.length_append] at hsize
  rw [← h, F.numStates_assemble]
  have htable := F.tableBits_length n t
  omega

theorem decode_of_parseFull_none (α : List Bool) (h : F.parseFull α = none) :
    F.decode α = F.fallback := by
  simp [decode, h]

theorem decode_of_guard_lt (α bits rest : List Bool) (hp : pairDecode α = some (bits, rest))
    (hg : rest.length < F.guard * (codeBitsNat bits + 1)) : F.decode α = F.fallback := by
  simp [decode, parseFull, hp, hg]

end CodeFormat

/-! ## Stage C: the one-tape instance and the transported public statements -/

lemma codeReadActionK_one_append {n : ℕ} (a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) (xs : List Bool) :
    codeReadActionK 1 n (actionBits a ++ xs) = some (a, xs) := by
  rw [actionBits_eq_actionBitsK]; exact codeReadActionK_append a xs

lemma codeReadActionK_one_sound {n : ℕ} (xs : List Bool) (a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1)))
    (rest : List Bool) (h : codeReadActionK 1 n xs = some (a, rest)) :
    xs = actionBits a ++ rest := by
  rw [actionBits_eq_actionBitsK]; exact codeReadActionK_sound xs a rest h

lemma codeEraseActionK_one (n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
    (codeReadActionK 1 n xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipAction n xs := by
  rw [codeEraseActionK, codeSkipAction_eq_codeSkipActionK]

/-- The one-work-tape format (`CodeTM`): nine records per state, guard `81`. -/
def oneTapeFormat : CodeFormat where
  Code := CodeTM
  Table := fun n => Fin (n + 1) → Option Bool → Option Bool → Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1))
  numStates := CodeTM.numStates
  assemble := fun n q t => ⟨n, ⟨q, fun s inp w => t s inp (w 0)⟩⟩
  serialize := CodeTM.serialize
  tableBits := fun {n} t => (List.finRange (n + 1)).flatMap fun q =>
    ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
      ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w =>
        actionBits (t q inp w)
  readTable := fun n =>
    codeReadVec (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 1 n))) (n + 1)
  skipTable := fun n =>
    codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipAction n) 3) 3) (n + 1)
  guard := 81
  fallback := codeFallback
  numStates_assemble := fun _ _ _ => rfl
  -- (D5) `serialize_assemble` is definitional for the one-tape format
  serialize_assemble := fun _ _ _ => rfl
  -- (D6) the dependent-structure eta step, as at the end of `codeParse_serialize_pad`
  assemble_surj := by
    rintro ⟨n, q, tr⟩
    refine ⟨n, q, fun s inp w => tr s inp (fun _ => w), ?_⟩
    show CodeTM.mk n ⟨q, fun s inp w => tr s inp (fun _ => w 0)⟩ = CodeTM.mk n ⟨q, tr⟩
    congr 1
    congr 1
    funext s inp w
    apply congrArg (tr s inp)
    funext i
    exact congrArg w (Subsingleton.elim _ _)
  readTable_append := fun _ _ _ =>
    codeReadVec_append _ _ (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _
      (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _ codeReadActionK_one_append _ _) _ _) _ _ _
  readTable_sound := fun _ _ _ _ h =>
    codeReadVec_sound _ _ (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _
      (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _ codeReadActionK_one_sound _ _ _) _ _ _)
      _ _ _ _ h
  readTable_erase := fun n xs => by
    have ht := codeEraseVec (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 1 n))) (n + 1) xs
    simp only [codeEraseSymbols, codeEraseActionK_one] at ht
    exact ht
  tableBits_length := fun n t => by
    have hr : ∀ a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1)), 9 ≤ (actionBits a).length := fun a => by
      rw [actionBits_eq_actionBitsK]; have := codeActionK_length a; omega
    have h := codeFlatMap_length (List.finRange (n + 1))
      (fun q => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
        ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w =>
          actionBits (t q inp w)) 81 (by
        intro q _
        have h := codeFlatMap_length ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
          (fun inp => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w =>
            actionBits (t q inp w)) 27 (by
              intro inp _
              simpa using codeFlatMap_length
                ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
                (fun w => actionBits (t q inp w)) 9 (fun _ _ => hr _))
        simpa using h)
    simpa using h

/-- Stand-in for the redefined public `codeDecode : List Bool → CodeTM`. -/
def probeDecode (xs : List Bool) : CodeTM := oneTapeFormat.decode xs

-- (D7a) the verbatim statement of `codeDecode_serialize_pad` (with `probeDecode`),
-- closed by term-mode instantiation, i.e. a defeq transport
theorem probe_decode_serialize_pad (M : CodeTM) (m : ℕ) :
    probeDecode (M.serialize ++ List.replicate m true) = M :=
  oneTapeFormat.decode_serialize_pad M m

-- (D7b) the new bodies are definitionally the current public bodies
example : oneTapeFormat.scan = codeScan := rfl
example : oneTapeFormat.canonical = codeCanonical := rfl

-- (D7c) the verbatim shape of `codeCanonical_eq`, against the existing `codeCanonical`
theorem probe_canonical_eq (xs : List Bool) : codeCanonical xs = (probeDecode xs).serialize :=
  oneTapeFormat.canonical_eq xs

-- (D7d) semantic check: the generic decoder is extensionally the current `codeDecode`
-- (from public facts only: both canonizer equations, and the injectivity of
-- serialization given by `codeDecode_serialize_pad` at `m = 0`)
theorem probeDecode_eq_codeDecode : probeDecode = codeDecode := by
  funext xs
  have h : (probeDecode xs).serialize = (codeDecode xs).serialize :=
    (probe_canonical_eq xs).symm.trans (codeCanonical_eq xs)
  have h1 := codeDecode_serialize_pad (probeDecode xs) 0
  have h2 := codeDecode_serialize_pad (codeDecode xs) 0
  simp only [List.replicate_zero, List.append_nil] at h1 h2
  exact (h1.symm.trans (congrArg codeDecode h)).trans h2

-- (R1, tested separately and resolved) Dot notation on an `F.Code`-typed term DOES
-- resolve.  `(oneTapeFormat.decode xs).numStates`, `(oneTapeFormat.decode xs).serialize`
-- and `(twoTapeFormat.decode α).toFinTM.ComputesInTime x [true] t` all elaborate.
-- Typed aliases are kept only so that the frozen signatures stay byte-identical.
example (xs : List Bool) : ℕ := (oneTapeFormat.decode xs).numStates

/-! ## Stage D: Level W (the 27-record two-work-tape table) and the two-tape instance -/

/-- Two-work-tape tables: input read by work-0 read by work-1 read, per state. -/
abbrev CodeTable2 (n : ℕ) : Type :=
  Fin (n + 1) → Option Bool → Option Bool → Option Bool → Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1))

def codeTable2Bits {n : ℕ} (t : CodeTable2 n) : List Bool :=
  (List.finRange (n + 1)).flatMap fun q =>
    ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
      ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₀ =>
        ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
          actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)

def codeReadTable2 (n : ℕ) : List Bool → Option (CodeTable2 n × List Bool) :=
  codeReadVec (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 2 n)))) (n + 1)

def codeSkipTable2 (n : ℕ) : List Bool → Option (List Bool) :=
  codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipActionK 2 n) 3) 3) 3)
    (n + 1)

-- (M)
lemma codeReadTable2_append {n : ℕ} (t : CodeTable2 n) (xs : List Bool) :
    codeReadTable2 n (codeTable2Bits t ++ xs) = some (t, xs) :=
  codeReadVec_append _ _ (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _
    (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _
      (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _ codeReadActionK_append _ _) _ _) _ _) _ _ _

-- (M)
lemma codeReadTable2_sound {n : ℕ} (xs : List Bool) (t : CodeTable2 n) (rest : List Bool)
    (h : codeReadTable2 n xs = some (t, rest)) : xs = codeTable2Bits t ++ rest :=
  codeReadVec_sound _ _ (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _
    (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _
      (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _ codeReadActionK_sound _ _ _) _ _ _) _ _ _)
    _ _ _ _ h

-- (M)
lemma codeEraseTable2 (n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
    (codeReadTable2 n xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipTable2 n xs := by
  have ht := codeEraseVec
    (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 2 n)))) (n + 1) xs
  simp only [codeEraseSymbols, codeEraseActionK] at ht
  exact ht

-- (M) the binding ZF-B grammar number: 351 = 27 · 13
lemma codeTable2Bits_length {n : ℕ} (t : CodeTable2 n) :
    351 * (n + 1) ≤ (codeTable2Bits t).length := by
  have hr : ∀ a : Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1)), 13 ≤ (actionBitsK a).length :=
    fun a => by have := codeActionK_length a; omega
  have h := codeFlatMap_length (List.finRange (n + 1))
    (fun q => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
      ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₀ =>
        ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
          actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 351 (by
      intro q _
      have h := codeFlatMap_length ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
        (fun inp => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₀ =>
          ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
            actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 117 (by
          intro inp _
          have h := codeFlatMap_length ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
            (fun w₀ => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
              actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 39 (by
                intro w₀ _
                simpa using codeFlatMap_length
                  ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
                  (fun w₁ => actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 13 (fun _ _ => hr _))
          simpa using h)
      simpa using h)
  simpa [codeTable2Bits] using h

/-- Probe-local stand-in for the private `zfBFallback` (identical literal). -/
def probeFallback2 : Code2TM :=
  ⟨0, ⟨0, fun _ _ _ => ⟨.zero, fun _ => (none, .zero), none, none⟩⟩⟩

/-- Stand-in for `zfBFallback_serialize_length` (identical proof). -/
lemma probeFallback2_serialize_length : probeFallback2.serialize.length = 354 := by
  unfold Code2TM.serialize
  dsimp only [probeFallback2]
  set_option maxRecDepth 10000 in
    norm_num [length_pairEncode, unaryFin, actionBits₂, signBits, optOptBoolBits,
      optBoolBits, optStateBits, List.finRange_succ, List.finRange_zero]

/-- The deterministic two-work-tape format (`Code2TM`). The fallback is the probe-local
`probeFallback2`, because `zfBFallback` is private to `Codes2Tape`. -/
def twoTapeFormat : CodeFormat where
  Code := Code2TM
  Table := CodeTable2
  numStates := Code2TM.numStates
  assemble := fun n q t => ⟨n, ⟨q, fun s inp w => t s inp (w 0) (w 1)⟩⟩
  serialize := Code2TM.serialize
  tableBits := fun t => codeTable2Bits t
  readTable := codeReadTable2
  skipTable := codeSkipTable2
  guard := 351
  fallback := probeFallback2
  numStates_assemble := fun _ _ _ => rfl
  -- (D5') propositional, via the record bridge plus `workPair` at the literals 0 and 1.
  -- Fallback if `simp` leaves a goal: `simp only [Code2TM.serialize, codeTable2Bits,
  -- actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK]` then `rfl`, using the definitional `workPair w₀ w₁ 0 ≡ w₀`
  -- reduction that `zfBParseFull_sound` already relies on.
  serialize_assemble := fun _ _ _ => by
    simp [Code2TM.serialize, codeTable2Bits, actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK, workPair]
  -- (D6') as at the end of `zfBParse_serialize_pad`
  assemble_surj := by
    rintro ⟨n, q, tr⟩
    refine ⟨n, q, fun s inp w₀ w₁ => tr s inp (workPair w₀ w₁), ?_⟩
    show Code2TM.mk n ⟨q, fun s inp w => tr s inp (workPair (w 0) (w 1))⟩ = Code2TM.mk n ⟨q, tr⟩
    congr 1
    congr 1
    funext s inp w
    apply congrArg (tr s inp)
    funext i
    fin_cases i <;> simp [workPair]
  readTable_append := fun _ t xs => codeReadTable2_append t xs
  readTable_sound := fun _ xs t rest h => codeReadTable2_sound xs t rest h
  readTable_erase := codeEraseTable2
  tableBits_length := fun _ t => codeTable2Bits_length t

/-- Stand-in for the retained typed alias `zfBDecode`. -/
def probeDecode2 (xs : List Bool) : Code2TM := twoTapeFormat.decode xs

-- (D9) the ZF-B3 private statements keep their exact text, re-proved by instantiation
-- (`zfB3_decode_length`, `zfB3_decode_states`, `zfB3_guard_failure`; `zfB3Answer_malformed`'s
-- `hd` step with the restated hypothesis `twoTapeFormat.parseFull α = none`)
example (α : List Bool) : (probeDecode2 α).serialize.length ≤ max α.length 354 := by
  have h := twoTapeFormat.serialize_decode_length_le α
  change (probeDecode2 α).serialize.length ≤ max α.length probeFallback2.serialize.length at h
  rwa [probeFallback2_serialize_length] at h

example (α : List Bool) : 351 * ((probeDecode2 α).numStates + 1) ≤ max α.length 354 := by
  have h := twoTapeFormat.numStates_decode_le α
  change 351 * ((probeDecode2 α).numStates + 1) ≤
    max α.length probeFallback2.serialize.length at h
  rwa [probeFallback2_serialize_length] at h

example (α bits rest : List Bool) (hp : pairDecode α = some (bits, rest))
    (hg : rest.length < 351 * (codeBitsNat bits + 1)) : probeDecode2 α = probeFallback2 :=
  twoTapeFormat.decode_of_guard_lt α bits rest hp hg

example (α : List Bool) (hparse : twoTapeFormat.parseFull α = none) :
    probeDecode2 α = probeFallback2 :=
  twoTapeFormat.decode_of_parseFull_none α hparse

-- (D7e) the two-tape transport, at the field type of `MachineCode2.decode_encode_pad`
example (M : Code2TM) (m : ℕ) :
    twoTapeFormat.decode (M.serialize ++ List.replicate m true) = M :=
  twoTapeFormat.decode_serialize_pad M m

/-! ## Stage E: the generic primitive-recursion layer and scheme assembly (MathlibBridge level) -/

lemma codePrimSkipWork : Primrec codeSkipWork :=
  Primrec.option_bind (codePrimSkipPair _) ((codePrimSkipPair _).comp Primrec.snd).to₂

-- (M) mirrors `codePrimSkipAction`, with one counted repetition
lemma codePrimSkipActionK (k : ℕ) : Primrec₂ (codeSkipActionK k) := by
  have hrep : Primrec (codeSkipRepeat codeSkipWork k) :=
    (codePrimRepeat (fun (_ : ℕ) => codeSkipWork) (codePrimSkipWork.comp Primrec.snd).to₂
      (fun _ => k) (Primrec.const k)).comp (Primrec.const 0) Primrec.id
  unfold codeSkipActionK
  apply Primrec.option_bind ((codePrimSkipPair _).comp Primrec.snd)
  change Primrec _
  apply Primrec.option_bind (hrep.comp Primrec.snd)
  change Primrec _
  apply Primrec.option_bind ((codePrimSkipPair _).comp Primrec.snd)
  change Primrec _
  exact codePrimSkipState.comp (Primrec.succ.comp
    (Primrec.fst.comp (Primrec.fst.comp (Primrec.fst.comp Primrec.fst)))) Primrec.snd

-- (D8) the generic `codePrimScan`: `81`/`351` become `F.guard`, the nested repeat becomes `h`
theorem CodeFormat.primrec_scan (F : CodeFormat) (h : Primrec₂ F.skipTable) :
    Primrec F.scan := by
  unfold CodeFormat.scan
  apply Primrec.option_bind codePrimPair
  change Primrec _
  apply Primrec.ite ((Primrec.eq.comp (Primrec.fst.comp Primrec.snd)
    (codePrimBits.comp (codePrimBitsNat.comp (Primrec.fst.comp Primrec.snd)))).not)
    (Primrec.const none)
  apply Primrec.ite (Primrec.nat_lt.comp
    (Primrec.list_length.comp (Primrec.snd.comp Primrec.snd))
    (Primrec.nat_mul.comp (Primrec.const F.guard)
      (Primrec.succ.comp (codePrimBitsNat.comp (Primrec.fst.comp Primrec.snd)))))
    (Primrec.const none)
  apply Primrec.option_bind (codePrimSkipFin.comp
    (Primrec.succ.comp (codePrimBitsNat.comp (Primrec.fst.comp Primrec.snd)))
    (Primrec.snd.comp Primrec.snd))
  change Primrec _
  apply Primrec.option_bind (h.comp
    (codePrimBitsNat.comp (Primrec.fst.comp (Primrec.snd.comp Primrec.fst))) Primrec.snd)
  change Primrec _
  exact Primrec.ite (Primrec.eq.comp (codePrimAll.comp Primrec.snd) (Primrec.const true))
    (Primrec.option_some.comp Primrec.snd) (Primrec.const none)

theorem CodeFormat.primrec_canonical (F : CodeFormat) (h : Primrec₂ F.skipTable) :
    Primrec F.canonical :=
  Primrec.option_casesOn (F.primrec_scan h) (Primrec.const (F.serialize F.fallback))
    (codePrimPrefix.comp Primrec.fst (Primrec.list_length.comp Primrec.snd))

theorem CodeFormat.exists_canonizer (F : CodeFormat) (h : Primrec₂ F.skipTable) :
    ∃ (M : FinTM Bool) (T : ℕ → ℕ),
      M.ComputesFunInTime (fun xs => F.serialize (F.decode xs)) T := by
  obtain ⟨M, T, hM⟩ := codePrim_machine F.canonical (F.primrec_canonical h)
  have he : F.canonical = fun xs => F.serialize (F.decode xs) := funext F.canonical_eq
  rw [he] at hM
  exact ⟨M, T, hM⟩

/-- Probe-local: the real `codePrimSkipAction` is private to `MathlibBridge`. -/
lemma codePrimSkipAction' : Primrec₂ codeSkipAction :=
  (codePrimSkipActionK 1).of_eq fun n xs => (codeSkipAction_eq_codeSkipActionK n xs).symm

lemma codePrimSkipTable1 : Primrec₂ oneTapeFormat.skipTable := by
  have h := codePrimRepeat _ (codePrimRepeat _ (codePrimRepeat _ codePrimSkipAction'
    (fun _ => 3) (Primrec.const 3)) (fun _ => 3) (Primrec.const 3)) (fun n => n + 1) Primrec.succ
  exact h

lemma codePrimSkipTable2 : Primrec₂ codeSkipTable2 := by
  have h := codePrimRepeat _ (codePrimRepeat _ (codePrimRepeat _ (codePrimRepeat _
    (codePrimSkipActionK 2) (fun _ => 3) (Primrec.const 3)) (fun _ => 3) (Primrec.const 3))
    (fun _ => 3) (Primrec.const 3)) (fun n => n + 1) Primrec.succ
  exact h

-- the existing private `codePrimScan`'s statement, from the generic layer (defeq to `codeScan`)
example : Primrec codeScan := oneTapeFormat.primrec_scan codePrimSkipTable1

-- the shape of `exists_effectiveMachineCode`'s literal, from the generic layer
theorem probe_effective : Nonempty EffectiveMachineCode := by
  obtain ⟨M, T, h⟩ := oneTapeFormat.exists_canonizer codePrimSkipTable1
  exact ⟨{
    encode := CodeTM.serialize
    decode := probeDecode
    decode_encode_pad := probe_decode_serialize_pad
    canonizer := M
    canonizerTime := T
    canonizer_computes := h }⟩

-- (D10) `MathlibBridge`'s private `codeCanonical_machine`, statement kept, as a one-line
-- wrapper (shown with `probeDecode`, which is extensionally `codeDecode`)
example : ∃ (M : FinTM Bool) (T : ℕ → ℕ),
    M.ComputesFunInTime (fun xs => (probeDecode xs).serialize) T :=
  oneTapeFormat.exists_canonizer codePrimSkipTable1

-- (D11) `exists_effectiveMachineCode2`'s intended new proof, with the alias as `decode`
theorem probe_effective2_alias : Nonempty EffectiveMachineCode2 := by
  obtain ⟨M, T, h⟩ := twoTapeFormat.exists_canonizer codePrimSkipTable2
  exact ⟨{
    encode := Code2TM.serialize
    decode := probeDecode2
    decode_encode_pad := twoTapeFormat.decode_serialize_pad
    canonizer := M
    canonizerTime := T
    canonizer_computes := h }⟩

-- the shape of `exists_effectiveMachineCode2`'s literal, from the generic layer
theorem probe_effective2 : Nonempty EffectiveMachineCode2 := by
  obtain ⟨M, T, h⟩ := twoTapeFormat.exists_canonizer codePrimSkipTable2
  exact ⟨{
    encode := Code2TM.serialize
    decode := twoTapeFormat.decode
    decode_encode_pad := twoTapeFormat.decode_serialize_pad
    canonizer := M
    canonizerTime := T
    canonizer_computes := h }⟩

end Turing

#print axioms Turing.probe_decode_serialize_pad
#print axioms Turing.probe_canonical_eq
#print axioms Turing.probeDecode_eq_codeDecode
#print axioms Turing.probe_effective
#print axioms Turing.probe_effective2
#print axioms Turing.probe_effective2_alias
#print axioms Turing.oneTapeFormat
#print axioms Turing.CodeFormat.serialize_decode_length_le
#print axioms Turing.CodeFormat.numStates_decode_le
#print axioms Turing.CodeFormat.decode_of_guard_lt
#print axioms Turing.CodeFormat.decode_of_parseFull_none
#print axioms Turing.twoTapeFormat
#print axioms Turing.CodeFormat.decode_serialize_pad
#print axioms Turing.CodeFormat.canonical_eq
#print axioms Turing.CodeFormat.exists_canonizer
