## ===== audits/evidence/ch34-t1/Item8MovePreShip.lean.txt =====

/-
Item 8 move probe (Q3): simulates moving `workPair` and `actionBits₂` verbatim from
`NDCodes.lean` (l. 98–110) down to the proposed `CodeFormat.lean`. The bridge
`actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK` then lives once, in `CodeFormat`, and the post-move remainder
of `NDCodes` and Codes2Tape's `Code2TM.serialize` elaborate against the moved definitions.
This file does NOT import `NDCodes`, `MathlibBridge` or `Codes2Tape`. It imports only what
the post-move `CodeFormat` (`CodeParser`) and `NDCodes` (`Nondeterministic`) would import,
and re-declares their content verbatim; sections are extracted from HEAD by `sed`.
-/
import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.CodeParser
import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.Nondeterministic
import Mathlib.Tactic.FinCases

set_option maxHeartbeats 0
set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
set_option autoImplicit false

namespace Turing

/-! ### Would sit in `CodeFormat.lean`: Level R's record serialization -/

/-- The fixed serialization of one record of a `k`-work-tape machine. -/
def actionBitsK {k n : ℕ} (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1))) : List Bool :=
  signBits a.inputTape ++
    (List.finRange k).flatMap
      (fun i => optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes i).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes i).2) ++
    optBoolBits a.output ++ optStateBits a.state

/-! ### Moved verbatim from `NDCodes.lean` l. 98–110 -/

/-- The work-symbol function reading `w₀` on tape `0` and `w₁` on tape `1`,
for the fixed table enumeration of `Turing.CodeNDTM.serialize`. -/
def workPair (w₀ w₁ : Option Bool) : Fin 2 → Option Bool :=
  fun j => if j = 0 then w₀ else w₁

/-- Serialization of one two-work-tape transition record: the input-head move,
then each work tape's optional write and move in tape order, then the emission
and the successor state — `Turing.actionBits` with a second work-tape record. -/
def actionBits₂ {n : ℕ} (a : Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) : List Bool :=
  signBits a.inputTape ++
    optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes 0).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes 0).2 ++
    optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes 1).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes 1).2 ++
    optBoolBits a.output ++ optStateBits a.state

/-! ### The bridge, once, in `CodeFormat.lean` -/

lemma actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK {n : ℕ} (a : Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) :
    actionBits₂ a = actionBitsK a := by
  simp [actionBits₂, actionBitsK, List.finRange_succ, List.append_assoc]

/-! ### Post-move `NDCodes.lean` remainder, verbatim (l. 81–96 and 112–163; the sorried
`exists_effectiveNDMachineCode`, l. 165–188, is omitted only to keep this probe sorry-free;
its statement mentions none of the moved names) -/

/-- The coded normal form of a nondeterministic machine: a binary-alphabet
NDTM with **two** work tapes and state space `Fin (numStates + 1)` (never
empty). Two work tapes, not one, because the guess-then-verify tape reduction
achieves linear overhead into two tapes (see the module docstring). Mirrors
`Turing.CodeTM`. [AB09, §1.4, §3.2] -/
structure CodeNDTM where
  /-- one less than the number of states (so the state space is never empty) -/
  numStates : ℕ
  /-- the underlying two-work-tape nondeterministic machine -/
  tm : NDTM 2 Bool (Fin (numStates + 1))

/-- The bundled machine of a coded nondeterministic machine. -/
def CodeNDTM.toFinNDTM (M : CodeNDTM) : FinNDTM Bool where
  k := 2
  State := Fin (M.numStates + 1)
  tm := M.tm

/-- The **fixed, scheme-independent** canonical serialization of a coded
nondeterministic machine, mirroring `Turing.CodeTM.serialize`: the state count,
the initial state, then the full two-table transition list in the fixed
enumeration order — the choice bit (`false` then `true`) outermost, then
states in `Fin` order, then the input read and the two work reads each over
`none`, `some false`, `some true`. This is the target format of
`Turing.EffectiveNDMachineCode.canonizer`. -/
def CodeNDTM.serialize (M : CodeNDTM) : List Bool :=
  pairEncode (Nat.bits M.numStates)
    (unaryFin M.tm.q₀ ++
      ([false, true] : List Bool).flatMap fun b =>
        (List.finRange (M.numStates + 1)).flatMap fun q =>
          ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
            ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₀ =>
              ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
                actionBits₂ (M.tm.tr b q inp (workPair w₀ w₁)))

/-- The algebraic laws of a representation scheme for coded nondeterministic
machines, mirroring `Turing.MachineCode` [AB09, §1.4]: a total decoding
(property 1), an encoding, and recovery under arbitrary `true`-padding
(property 2 — every machine has infinitely many representations, which the
lazy diagonalization of [AB09, Theorem 3.2] uses to pick large indices). -/
structure NDMachineCode where
  /-- encode a machine as a binary string, `⌞N⌟` -/
  encode : CodeNDTM → List Bool
  /-- decode any binary string to a machine (total by type: property 1) -/
  decode : List Bool → CodeNDTM
  /-- a code followed by any amount of `true`-padding decodes to the machine
  (property 2: infinitely many representations) -/
  decode_encode_pad : ∀ M m, decode (encode M ++ List.replicate m true) = M

/-- Decoding a code recovers the machine ([AB09, §1.4]; padding by zero
symbols). Skeleton-time proof, mirroring `Turing.MachineCode.decode_encode`. -/
theorem NDMachineCode.decode_encode (c : NDMachineCode) (M : CodeNDTM) :
    c.decode (c.encode M) = M := by
  simpa using c.decode_encode_pad M 0

/-- An *effective* representation scheme for nondeterministic machines: the
algebraic laws together with a (deterministic) machine of this development
computing the fixed serialization of the decoded machine — the mirror of
`Turing.EffectiveMachineCode`, with the same Argument-A rationale: the target
`Turing.CodeNDTM.serialize` is scheme-independent, so a scheme whose `decode`
has noncomputable meaning admits no canonizer. -/
structure EffectiveNDMachineCode extends NDMachineCode where
  /-- a machine computing the fixed serialization of the decoded machine -/
  canonizer : FinTM Bool
  /-- the canonizer's time bound (arbitrary here; universal-machine constants
  absorb its value at each fixed code) -/
  canonizerTime : ℕ → ℕ
  /-- the canonizer computes `serialize ∘ decode` -/
  canonizer_computes :
    canonizer.ComputesFunInTime (fun α => (decode α).serialize) canonizerTime

/-! ### Codes2Tape's `Code2TM` and `Code2TM.serialize`, verbatim (l. 78–113) -/

/-- The coded normal form of a deterministic machine with **two** work
tapes: a binary-alphabet machine with state space `Fin (numStates + 1)`
(never empty). Two work tapes, not one, because the Hennie-Stearns
conversion lands there at `O(T log T)` and the two-tape universal machine
(its consumer) runs such codes at linear overhead — the whole point of
strengthening past `Turing.CodeTM`'s square. Mirrors `Turing.CodeTM` and
`Turing.CodeNDTM`. [AB09, §1.4, §1.7] -/
structure Code2TM where
  /-- one less than the number of states (so the state space is never empty) -/
  numStates : ℕ
  /-- the underlying two-work-tape deterministic machine -/
  tm : MultiTapeTM 2 Bool (Fin (numStates + 1))

/-- The bundled machine of a coded two-tape machine. -/
def Code2TM.toFinTM (M : Code2TM) : FinTM Bool where
  k := 2
  State := Fin (M.numStates + 1)
  tm := M.tm

/-- The **fixed, scheme-independent** canonical serialization, mirroring
`Turing.CodeTM.serialize` and `Turing.CodeNDTM.serialize` over the same
`Turing.actionBits₂` record: the state count, the initial state, then the
full transition table in the fixed enumeration order — states in `Fin`
order, then the input read and the two work reads each over `none`,
`some false`, `some true` (27 records per state; the nondeterministic
table's outermost choice bit is absent). This is the target format of
`Turing.EffectiveMachineCode2.canonizer` and the input format of the
two-work-tape universal machine. -/
def Code2TM.serialize (M : Code2TM) : List Bool :=
  pairEncode (Nat.bits M.numStates)
    (unaryFin M.tm.q₀ ++
      (List.finRange (M.numStates + 1)).flatMap fun q =>
        ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
          ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₀ =>
            ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
              actionBits₂ (M.tm.tr q inp (workPair w₀ w₁)))

/-! ### The 354-bit regression still unfolds the moved `actionBits₂` by name
(the `zfBFallback_serialize_length` proof, Codes2Tape l. 184–189, on the same literal) -/

def probeFallback2 : Code2TM :=
  ⟨0, ⟨0, fun _ _ _ => ⟨.zero, fun _ => (none, .zero), none, none⟩⟩⟩

example : probeFallback2.serialize.length = 354 := by
  unfold Code2TM.serialize
  dsimp only [probeFallback2]
  set_option maxRecDepth 10000 in
    norm_num [length_pairEncode, unaryFin, actionBits₂, signBits, optOptBoolBits,
      optBoolBits, optStateBits, List.finRange_succ, List.finRange_zero]

end Turing

#print axioms Turing.actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK
#print axioms Turing.NDMachineCode.decode_encode

## ===== audits/evidence/ch34-t1/Item8ContextPreShip.lean.txt =====

/-
Item 8 CodeFormat-context probe: the content proposed for the new
`TuringMachine/CodeFormat.lean` (Level R with the moved `workPair`/`actionBits₂` and all
three bridges, Level T with the B5-facing corollaries, the one-tape instance and its
checks, Level W), elaborated under exactly the proposed import, `CodeParser` alone.
It does not import `NDCodes`; the two moved definitions are extracted verbatim from
`NDCodes.lean` l. 98-110 by script.  The stage text is the main probe's (`item8-probe.lean`).
-/
import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.CodeParser

set_option maxHeartbeats 0
set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
set_option autoImplicit false

namespace Turing

/-! ## Moved verbatim from `NDCodes.lean` l. 98-110 -/

/-- The work-symbol function reading `w₀` on tape `0` and `w₁` on tape `1`,
for the fixed table enumeration of `Turing.CodeNDTM.serialize`. -/
def workPair (w₀ w₁ : Option Bool) : Fin 2 → Option Bool :=
  fun j => if j = 0 then w₀ else w₁

/-- Serialization of one two-work-tape transition record: the input-head move,
then each work tape's optional write and move in tape order, then the emission
and the successor state — `Turing.actionBits` with a second work-tape record. -/
def actionBits₂ {n : ℕ} (a : Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) : List Bool :=
  signBits a.inputTape ++
    optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes 0).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes 0).2 ++
    optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes 1).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes 1).2 ++
    optBoolBits a.output ++ optStateBits a.state

/-! ## Stage A: Level R, the transition record generic in the work-tape count `k` -/

/-- The fixed serialization of one record of a `k`-work-tape machine: the input move,
each work tape's optional write and move in tape order, the emission, the successor. -/
def actionBitsK {k n : ℕ} (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1))) : List Bool :=
  signBits a.inputTape ++
    (List.finRange k).flatMap
      (fun i => optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes i).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes i).2) ++
    optBoolBits a.output ++ optStateBits a.state

/-- Read one work-tape field pair: the optional write, then the move. -/
def codeReadWork (xs : List Bool) :
    Option ((Option (Option Bool) × SignType) × List Bool) := do
  let (wr, xs) ← codeReadWrite xs
  let (wm, xs) ← codeReadSign xs
  pure ((wr, wm), xs)

/-- Read one `k`-work-tape record. The work-tape actions are read as a
`Fin k`-indexed vector, so the record reconstructs `Action.workTapes` exactly. -/
def codeReadActionK (k n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
    Option (Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1)) × List Bool) := do
  let (im, xs) ← codeReadSign xs
  let (ws, xs) ← codeReadVec codeReadWork k xs
  let (out, xs) ← codeReadOutput xs
  let (q, xs) ← codeReadState (n + 1) xs
  pure (⟨im, ws, out, q⟩, xs)

/-- The erased work-pair reader. -/
def codeSkipWork (xs : List Bool) : Option (List Bool) :=
  (codeSkipPair (fun _ _ => true) xs).bind (codeSkipPair (fun a b => a || !b))

/-- The erased `k`-work-tape record reader. -/
def codeSkipActionK (k n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) : Option (List Bool) := do
  let xs ← codeSkipPair (fun a b => a || !b) xs
  let xs ← codeSkipRepeat codeSkipWork k xs
  let xs ← codeSkipPair (fun a b => a || !b) xs
  codeSkipState (n + 1) xs

-- (M)
lemma codeReadWork_append (p : Option (Option Bool) × SignType) (xs : List Bool) :
    codeReadWork ((optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2) ++ xs) = some (p, xs) := by
  rcases p with ⟨wr, wm⟩
  simp only [codeReadWork, List.append_assoc, codeReadWrite_append, codeReadSign_append,
    bind, Option.bind, pure]

-- (D1) the vector read reconstructs `workTapes` with no `Subsingleton`/`fin_cases` step
lemma codeReadActionK_append {k n : ℕ} (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1))) (xs : List Bool) :
    codeReadActionK k n (actionBitsK a ++ xs) = some (a, xs) := by
  rcases a with ⟨im, ws, out, q⟩
  have hv : ∀ ys, codeReadVec codeReadWork k
      ((List.finRange k).flatMap (fun i => optOptBoolBits (ws i).1 ++ signBits (ws i).2) ++ ys) =
        some (ws, ys) :=
    codeReadVec_append codeReadWork (fun p => optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2)
      codeReadWork_append k ws
  simp only [actionBitsK, List.append_assoc, codeReadActionK, codeReadSign_append, hv,
    codeReadOutput_append, codeReadState_append, bind, Option.bind, pure]

-- (M)
lemma codeReadWork_sound (xs : List Bool) (p : Option (Option Bool) × SignType)
    (rest : List Bool) (h : codeReadWork xs = some (p, rest)) :
    xs = (optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2) ++ rest := by
  simp only [codeReadWork, bind, Option.bind_eq_some_iff] at h
  obtain ⟨⟨wr, r₁⟩, h₁, ⟨⟨wm, r₂⟩, h₂, h⟩⟩ := h
  simp only [pure, Option.some.injEq, Prod.mk.injEq] at h
  rcases h with ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
  rw [codeReadWrite_sound xs wr r₁ h₁, codeReadSign_sound r₁ wm r₂ h₂]
  simp [List.append_assoc]

-- (M)
lemma codeReadActionK_sound {k n : ℕ} (xs : List Bool) (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1)))
    (rest : List Bool) (h : codeReadActionK k n xs = some (a, rest)) :
    xs = actionBitsK a ++ rest := by
  simp only [codeReadActionK, bind, Option.bind_eq_some_iff] at h
  obtain ⟨⟨im, r₁⟩, h₁, ⟨⟨ws, r₂⟩, h₂, ⟨⟨out, r₃⟩, h₃, ⟨⟨q, r₄⟩, h₄, h⟩⟩⟩⟩ := h
  simp only [pure, Option.some.injEq, Prod.mk.injEq] at h
  rcases h with ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
  rw [codeReadSign_sound xs im r₁ h₁,
    codeReadVec_sound codeReadWork (fun p => optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2)
      codeReadWork_sound k r₁ ws r₂ h₂,
    codeReadOutput_sound r₂ out r₃ h₃, codeReadState_sound r₃ q r₄ h₄]
  simp [actionBitsK, List.append_assoc]

-- (M) mirrors the last two steps of `codeEraseAction`
lemma codeEraseWork (xs : List Bool) : (codeReadWork xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipWork xs := by
  simp only [codeReadWork, codeSkipWork, bind, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def, pure,
    Option.map_some]
  rw [← codeEraseWrite xs, ← codeErase_bind]
  congr 1; funext p
  simpa only [Option.map_eq_bind, Function.comp_def] using codeEraseSign p.2

-- (M) mirrors `codeEraseAction`, with one vector step
lemma codeEraseActionK (k n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
    (codeReadActionK k n xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipActionK k n xs := by
  have hv (ys : List Bool) :
      (codeReadVec codeReadWork k ys).map Prod.snd = codeSkipRepeat codeSkipWork k ys := by
    rw [codeEraseVec]
    exact congrArg (fun r => codeSkipRepeat r k ys) (funext codeEraseWork)
  simp only [codeReadActionK, codeSkipActionK, bind, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def, pure,
    Option.map_some]
  rw [← codeEraseSign xs, ← codeErase_bind]
  congr 1; funext p
  rw [← hv p.2, ← codeErase_bind]
  congr 1; funext p
  rw [← codeEraseOutput p.2, ← codeErase_bind]
  congr 1; funext p
  simpa only [Option.map_eq_bind, Function.comp_def] using codeEraseState (n + 1) p.2

-- (M) generalizes `codeAction_length` (9 = 4·1 + 5) and `zfBAction_length` (13 = 4·2 + 5)
lemma codeActionK_length {k n : ℕ} (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1))) :
    4 * k + 5 ≤ (actionBitsK a).length := by
  have hs (s : SignType) : (signBits s).length = 2 := by cases s <;> rfl
  have ho (b : Option Bool) : (optBoolBits b).length = 2 := by
    rcases b with _ | b
    · rfl
    · cases b <;> rfl
  have hw (b : Option (Option Bool)) : (optOptBoolBits b).length = 2 := by
    rcases b with _ | (_ | b)
    · rfl
    · rfl
    · cases b <;> rfl
  have hq : 1 ≤ (optStateBits a.state).length := by
    cases a.state <;> simp [optStateBits, unaryFin]
  have hv : 4 * k ≤ ((List.finRange k).flatMap fun i =>
      optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes i).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes i).2).length := by
    simpa using codeFlatMap_length (List.finRange k)
      (fun i => optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes i).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes i).2) 4
      (fun i _ => by simp [hs, hw])
  simp only [actionBitsK, List.length_append, hs, ho]
  omega

-- (D2) the frozen one-tape record is the `k = 1` record (propositionally)
lemma actionBits_eq_actionBitsK {n : ℕ} (a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) :
    actionBits a = actionBitsK a := by
  simp [actionBits, actionBitsK, List.finRange_succ, List.append_assoc]

-- (D2) the frozen two-tape record is the `k = 2` record (propositionally).  In the real
-- change it lives in Codes2Tape (private), because `actionBits₂` is NDCodes-owned.
lemma actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK {n : ℕ} (a : Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) :
    actionBits₂ a = actionBitsK a := by
  simp [actionBits₂, actionBitsK, List.finRange_succ, List.append_assoc]

-- (D3) the frozen public one-tape skipper agrees with the generic one; this keeps
-- `codeSkipAction`'s body (and `MathlibBridge`'s `unfold codeSkipAction`) untouched
lemma codeSkipAction_eq_codeSkipActionK (n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
    codeSkipAction n xs = codeSkipActionK 1 n xs := by
  have h1 (ys : List Bool) : codeSkipRepeat codeSkipWork 1 ys = codeSkipWork ys := by
    cases h : codeSkipWork ys <;> simp [codeSkipRepeat, h]
  simp only [codeSkipAction, codeSkipActionK, h1, codeSkipWork, bind, Option.bind_assoc]

/-! ## Stage B: Level T, the format-parameterized top layer -/

/-- A machine-code format over the shared header grammar
`pairEncode (Nat.bits n) (unaryFin q₀ ++ table)`: the coded machines, their tables, the
table grammar (writer, reader, erased reader, per-state minimum length) and the
fallback, with the laws that the generic parser proofs consume.
(The field is `assemble`, not `mk`: a field named `mk` would clash with the
structure's default constructor `CodeFormat.mk`.) -/
structure CodeFormat where
  /-- the coded machines -/
  Code : Type
  /-- the transition table of a machine with `n + 1` states -/
  Table : ℕ → Type
  /-- one less than the number of states -/
  numStates : Code → ℕ
  /-- assemble a machine from its state count, initial state and table -/
  assemble : (n : ℕ) → Fin (n + 1) → Table n → Code
  /-- the format's fixed serialization -/
  serialize : Code → List Bool
  /-- the fixed serialization of a table -/
  tableBits : {n : ℕ} → Table n → List Bool
  /-- the table reader -/
  readTable : (n : ℕ) → List Bool → Option (Table n × List Bool)
  /-- the erased table reader -/
  skipTable : ℕ → List Bool → Option (List Bool)
  /-- the minimum number of table bits per state (the up-front guard constant) -/
  guard : ℕ
  /-- the machine denoted by every malformed string -/
  fallback : Code
  numStates_assemble : ∀ n q t, numStates (assemble n q t) = n
  serialize_assemble : ∀ n q (t : Table n),
    serialize (assemble n q t) = pairEncode n.bits (unaryFin q ++ tableBits t)
  assemble_surj : ∀ M, ∃ n q t, assemble n q t = M
  readTable_append : ∀ n (t : Table n) xs, readTable n (tableBits t ++ xs) = some (t, xs)
  readTable_sound : ∀ n xs (t : Table n) rest,
    readTable n xs = some (t, rest) → xs = tableBits t ++ rest
  readTable_erase : ∀ n xs, (readTable n xs).map Prod.snd = skipTable n xs
  tableBits_length : ∀ n (t : Table n), guard * (n + 1) ≤ (tableBits t).length

namespace CodeFormat

variable (F : CodeFormat)

/-- Parse the header and the table, keeping the unconsumed padding. The length guard
precedes table recursion; the suffix must be all `true`. -/
def parseFull (xs : List Bool) : Option (F.Code × List Bool) := do
  let (bits, rest) ← pairDecode xs
  let n := codeBitsNat bits
  if bits ≠ n.bits then none else do
    if F.guard * (n + 1) > rest.length then none else do
      let (q, rest) ← codeReadFin (n + 1) rest
      let (table, rest) ← F.readTable n rest
      if rest.all id then pure (F.assemble n q table, rest) else none

/-- Total decoding: every malformed string denotes the fallback. -/
def decode (xs : List Bool) : F.Code := ((F.parseFull xs).map Prod.fst).getD F.fallback

/-- The erased parser: same guard and padding test, returning only the suffix. -/
def scan (xs : List Bool) : Option (List Bool) := do
  let (bits, rest) ← pairDecode xs
  let n := codeBitsNat bits
  if bits ≠ n.bits then none else do
    if F.guard * (n + 1) > rest.length then none else do
      let rest ← codeSkipFin (n + 1) rest
      let rest ← F.skipTable n rest
      if rest.all id then pure rest else none

/-- The consumed prefix on scanner success, the fallback's serialization otherwise. -/
def canonical (xs : List Bool) : List Bool :=
  (F.scan xs).casesOn (F.serialize F.fallback) fun tail => xs.take (xs.length - tail.length)

-- (D4) the generic round trip; `omega` must treat `F.guard * (n + 1)` as an atom
theorem parseFull_serialize_pad (M : F.Code) (m : ℕ) :
    F.parseFull (F.serialize M ++ List.replicate m true) =
      some (M, List.replicate m true) := by
  obtain ⟨n, q, t, rfl⟩ := F.assemble_surj M
  have hp (a b c : List Bool) : pairEncode a b ++ c = pairEncode a (b ++ c) := by
    simp [pairEncode, List.append_assoc]
  rw [F.serialize_assemble, hp]
  unfold parseFull
  rw [pairDecode_pairEncode]
  dsimp only [bind, Option.bind]
  rw [codeBitsNat_bits]
  simp only [ne_eq, not_true_eq_false, ↓reduceIte]
  have hlen := F.tableBits_length n t
  simp only [List.length_append, List.length_replicate] at *
  rw [if_neg (by omega)]
  simp only [List.append_assoc, codeReadFin_append, F.readTable_append,
    List.all_replicate, id_eq, ite_self, ↓reduceIte, pure]

theorem decode_serialize_pad (M : F.Code) (m : ℕ) :
    F.decode (F.serialize M ++ List.replicate m true) = M := by
  simp only [decode, parseFull_serialize_pad, Option.map_some, Option.getD_some]

-- (M) mirrors `zfBParseFull_sound`
theorem parseFull_sound (xs : List Bool) (M : F.Code) (tail : List Bool)
    (h : F.parseFull xs = some (M, tail)) : xs = F.serialize M ++ tail := by
  unfold parseFull at h
  obtain ⟨⟨bits, rest⟩, hp, h⟩ := Option.bind_eq_some_iff.mp h
  dsimp only at h
  split at h
  · contradiction
  next hb =>
    have hb : bits = (codeBitsNat bits).bits := not_not.mp hb
    split at h
    · contradiction
    next _ =>
      simp only [bind, Option.bind_eq_some_iff] at h
      obtain ⟨⟨q, r₁⟩, hq, ⟨⟨table, r₂⟩, ht, h⟩⟩ := h
      split at h
      next hpad =>
        simp only [pure, Option.some.injEq, Prod.mk.injEq] at h
        rcases h with ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
        have htable := F.readTable_sound _ _ _ _ ht
        dsimp only at htable
        rw [F.serialize_assemble, eq_pairEncode_of_pairDecode xs bits rest hp,
          codeReadFin_sound rest q r₁ hq, htable]
        simp only [pairEncode, List.append_assoc]
        congr 1
        exact congrArg (List.flatMap fun b : Bool => [b, b]) hb
      · contradiction

-- (M) mirrors `zfBScan_full`
theorem scan_full (xs : List Bool) : F.scan xs = (F.parseFull xs).map Prod.snd := by
  simp only [scan, parseFull, bind, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def]
  congr 1
  funext p
  dsimp only
  split <;> try simp only [Option.map_none]
  split <;> try simp only [Option.map_none, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def]
  have h (q : Fin (codeBitsNat p.1 + 1) × List Bool) (t : F.Table (codeBitsNat p.1) × List Bool) :
      (if t.2.all id then some (F.assemble (codeBitsNat p.1) q.1 t.1, t.2) else none).map
          Prod.snd = (if t.2.all id then some t.2 else none) := by split <;> rfl
  simp only [pure, h]
  rw [← codeEraseFin _ _, ← codeErase_bind]
  congr 1; funext q
  rw [← F.readTable_erase, ← codeErase_bind]

-- (M) mirrors `zfBCanonical_eq`; `parse_full` disappears because `decode` is defined
-- from `parseFull` directly
theorem canonical_eq (xs : List Bool) : F.canonical xs = F.serialize (F.decode xs) := by
  unfold canonical decode
  rw [scan_full]
  cases h : F.parseFull xs with
  | none => rfl
  | some p =>
    rcases p with ⟨M, tail⟩
    simp only [Option.map_some, Option.getD_some]
    rw [F.parseFull_sound xs M tail h]
    simp

-- (B5-facing corollaries; generalize ZF-B3's `zfB3_decode_length`/`_states`/`_guard_failure`)
theorem serialize_decode_length_le (α : List Bool) :
    (F.serialize (F.decode α)).length ≤ max α.length (F.serialize F.fallback).length := by
  rw [← F.canonical_eq]
  unfold canonical
  cases F.scan α with
  | none => exact Nat.le_max_right _ _
  | some tail => exact (List.length_take_le' _ _).trans (Nat.le_max_left _ _)

theorem numStates_decode_le (α : List Bool) :
    F.guard * (F.numStates (F.decode α) + 1) ≤
      max α.length (F.serialize F.fallback).length := by
  have hsize := F.serialize_decode_length_le α
  obtain ⟨n, q, t, h⟩ := F.assemble_surj (F.decode α)
  rw [← h, F.serialize_assemble, length_pairEncode, List.length_append] at hsize
  rw [← h, F.numStates_assemble]
  have htable := F.tableBits_length n t
  omega

theorem decode_of_parseFull_none (α : List Bool) (h : F.parseFull α = none) :
    F.decode α = F.fallback := by
  simp [decode, h]

theorem decode_of_guard_lt (α bits rest : List Bool) (hp : pairDecode α = some (bits, rest))
    (hg : rest.length < F.guard * (codeBitsNat bits + 1)) : F.decode α = F.fallback := by
  simp [decode, parseFull, hp, hg]

end CodeFormat

/-! ## Stage C: the one-tape instance and the transported public statements -/

lemma codeReadActionK_one_append {n : ℕ} (a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) (xs : List Bool) :
    codeReadActionK 1 n (actionBits a ++ xs) = some (a, xs) := by
  rw [actionBits_eq_actionBitsK]; exact codeReadActionK_append a xs

lemma codeReadActionK_one_sound {n : ℕ} (xs : List Bool) (a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1)))
    (rest : List Bool) (h : codeReadActionK 1 n xs = some (a, rest)) :
    xs = actionBits a ++ rest := by
  rw [actionBits_eq_actionBitsK]; exact codeReadActionK_sound xs a rest h

lemma codeEraseActionK_one (n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
    (codeReadActionK 1 n xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipAction n xs := by
  rw [codeEraseActionK, codeSkipAction_eq_codeSkipActionK]

/-- The one-work-tape format (`CodeTM`): nine records per state, guard `81`. -/
def oneTapeFormat : CodeFormat where
  Code := CodeTM
  Table := fun n => Fin (n + 1) → Option Bool → Option Bool → Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1))
  numStates := CodeTM.numStates
  assemble := fun n q t => ⟨n, ⟨q, fun s inp w => t s inp (w 0)⟩⟩
  serialize := CodeTM.serialize
  tableBits := fun {n} t => (List.finRange (n + 1)).flatMap fun q =>
    ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
      ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w =>
        actionBits (t q inp w)
  readTable := fun n =>
    codeReadVec (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 1 n))) (n + 1)
  skipTable := fun n =>
    codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipAction n) 3) 3) (n + 1)
  guard := 81
  fallback := codeFallback
  numStates_assemble := fun _ _ _ => rfl
  -- (D5) `serialize_assemble` is definitional for the one-tape format
  serialize_assemble := fun _ _ _ => rfl
  -- (D6) the dependent-structure eta step, as at the end of `codeParse_serialize_pad`
  assemble_surj := by
    rintro ⟨n, q, tr⟩
    refine ⟨n, q, fun s inp w => tr s inp (fun _ => w), ?_⟩
    show CodeTM.mk n ⟨q, fun s inp w => tr s inp (fun _ => w 0)⟩ = CodeTM.mk n ⟨q, tr⟩
    congr 1
    congr 1
    funext s inp w
    apply congrArg (tr s inp)
    funext i
    exact congrArg w (Subsingleton.elim _ _)
  readTable_append := fun _ _ _ =>
    codeReadVec_append _ _ (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _
      (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _ codeReadActionK_one_append _ _) _ _) _ _ _
  readTable_sound := fun _ _ _ _ h =>
    codeReadVec_sound _ _ (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _
      (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _ codeReadActionK_one_sound _ _ _) _ _ _)
      _ _ _ _ h
  readTable_erase := fun n xs => by
    have ht := codeEraseVec (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 1 n))) (n + 1) xs
    simp only [codeEraseSymbols, codeEraseActionK_one] at ht
    exact ht
  tableBits_length := fun n t => by
    have hr : ∀ a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1)), 9 ≤ (actionBits a).length := fun a => by
      rw [actionBits_eq_actionBitsK]; have := codeActionK_length a; omega
    have h := codeFlatMap_length (List.finRange (n + 1))
      (fun q => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
        ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w =>
          actionBits (t q inp w)) 81 (by
        intro q _
        have h := codeFlatMap_length ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
          (fun inp => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w =>
            actionBits (t q inp w)) 27 (by
              intro inp _
              simpa using codeFlatMap_length
                ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
                (fun w => actionBits (t q inp w)) 9 (fun _ _ => hr _))
        simpa using h)
    simpa using h

/-- Stand-in for the redefined public `codeDecode : List Bool → CodeTM`. -/
def probeDecode (xs : List Bool) : CodeTM := oneTapeFormat.decode xs

-- (D7a) the verbatim statement of `codeDecode_serialize_pad` (with `probeDecode`),
-- closed by term-mode instantiation, i.e. a defeq transport
theorem probe_decode_serialize_pad (M : CodeTM) (m : ℕ) :
    probeDecode (M.serialize ++ List.replicate m true) = M :=
  oneTapeFormat.decode_serialize_pad M m

-- (D7b) the new bodies are definitionally the current public bodies
example : oneTapeFormat.scan = codeScan := rfl
example : oneTapeFormat.canonical = codeCanonical := rfl

-- (D7c) the verbatim shape of `codeCanonical_eq`, against the existing `codeCanonical`
theorem probe_canonical_eq (xs : List Bool) : codeCanonical xs = (probeDecode xs).serialize :=
  oneTapeFormat.canonical_eq xs

-- (D7d) semantic check: the generic decoder is extensionally the current `codeDecode`
-- (from public facts only: both canonizer equations, and the injectivity of
-- serialization given by `codeDecode_serialize_pad` at `m = 0`)
theorem probeDecode_eq_codeDecode : probeDecode = codeDecode := by
  funext xs
  have h : (probeDecode xs).serialize = (codeDecode xs).serialize :=
    (probe_canonical_eq xs).symm.trans (codeCanonical_eq xs)
  have h1 := codeDecode_serialize_pad (probeDecode xs) 0
  have h2 := codeDecode_serialize_pad (codeDecode xs) 0
  simp only [List.replicate_zero, List.append_nil] at h1 h2
  exact (h1.symm.trans (congrArg codeDecode h)).trans h2

-- (R1, tested separately and resolved) Dot notation on an `F.Code`-typed term DOES
-- resolve.  `(oneTapeFormat.decode xs).numStates`, `(oneTapeFormat.decode xs).serialize`
-- and `(twoTapeFormat.decode α).toFinTM.ComputesInTime x [true] t` all elaborate.
-- Typed aliases are kept only so that the frozen signatures stay byte-identical.
example (xs : List Bool) : ℕ := (oneTapeFormat.decode xs).numStates

/-! ## Stage D: Level W (the 27-record two-work-tape table) and the two-tape instance -/

/-- Two-work-tape tables: input read by work-0 read by work-1 read, per state. -/
abbrev CodeTable2 (n : ℕ) : Type :=
  Fin (n + 1) → Option Bool → Option Bool → Option Bool → Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1))

def codeTable2Bits {n : ℕ} (t : CodeTable2 n) : List Bool :=
  (List.finRange (n + 1)).flatMap fun q =>
    ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
      ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₀ =>
        ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
          actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)

def codeReadTable2 (n : ℕ) : List Bool → Option (CodeTable2 n × List Bool) :=
  codeReadVec (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 2 n)))) (n + 1)

def codeSkipTable2 (n : ℕ) : List Bool → Option (List Bool) :=
  codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipActionK 2 n) 3) 3) 3)
    (n + 1)

-- (M)
lemma codeReadTable2_append {n : ℕ} (t : CodeTable2 n) (xs : List Bool) :
    codeReadTable2 n (codeTable2Bits t ++ xs) = some (t, xs) :=
  codeReadVec_append _ _ (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _
    (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _
      (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _ codeReadActionK_append _ _) _ _) _ _) _ _ _

-- (M)
lemma codeReadTable2_sound {n : ℕ} (xs : List Bool) (t : CodeTable2 n) (rest : List Bool)
    (h : codeReadTable2 n xs = some (t, rest)) : xs = codeTable2Bits t ++ rest :=
  codeReadVec_sound _ _ (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _
    (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _
      (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _ codeReadActionK_sound _ _ _) _ _ _) _ _ _)
    _ _ _ _ h

-- (M)
lemma codeEraseTable2 (n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
    (codeReadTable2 n xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipTable2 n xs := by
  have ht := codeEraseVec
    (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 2 n)))) (n + 1) xs
  simp only [codeEraseSymbols, codeEraseActionK] at ht
  exact ht

-- (M) the binding ZF-B grammar number: 351 = 27 · 13
lemma codeTable2Bits_length {n : ℕ} (t : CodeTable2 n) :
    351 * (n + 1) ≤ (codeTable2Bits t).length := by
  have hr : ∀ a : Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1)), 13 ≤ (actionBitsK a).length :=
    fun a => by have := codeActionK_length a; omega
  have h := codeFlatMap_length (List.finRange (n + 1))
    (fun q => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
      ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₀ =>
        ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
          actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 351 (by
      intro q _
      have h := codeFlatMap_length ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
        (fun inp => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₀ =>
          ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
            actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 117 (by
          intro inp _
          have h := codeFlatMap_length ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
            (fun w₀ => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
              actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 39 (by
                intro w₀ _
                simpa using codeFlatMap_length
                  ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
                  (fun w₁ => actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 13 (fun _ _ => hr _))
          simpa using h)
      simpa using h)
  simpa [codeTable2Bits] using h


end Turing

#print axioms Turing.oneTapeFormat
#print axioms Turing.probeDecode_eq_codeDecode
#print axioms Turing.codeTable2Bits_length
#print axioms Turing.actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK
#print axioms Turing.CodeFormat.numStates_decode_le

# 12.2c tranche T1, item 8 (CH34-D2) — integration record (2026-10-10)

The format-parameterized machine-code parse layer (`briefs/ch34-t1-item8.md`) was
delivered complete (Tasks 1 and 2). It is integrated on branch
`integrate/12.2c-t1-codeformat` by `git am -3`, with Codex authorship, on top of
`b4be0717` (epoch C1 + S1a records):

| Commit | Content |
|---|---|
| `603b3a35` | Task 1: new `CodeFormat.lean` (Levels R/T/W, `oneTapeFormat`, the seven moved publics); CodeParser, MathlibBridge, NDCodes, facade |

`audits/12.2c-agent-reports/t1-codeformat-REPORT.md`.

## ===== audits/12.2c-agent-reports/t1-codeformat-REPORT.md =====

# Item 8 (CH34-D2): machine-code format refactor

Tasks 1 and 2 complete. The source replay, statement freeze, public-axiom comparison,
duplication gates and lint pass. **One required legacy audit program fails identically
at the base and final commits** because it expects an already-proved theorem to
remain admitted; see the verification table. This is not an unconditional pass of
that legacy program.

## Provenance and scope

- Repository: https://github.com/Shilun-Allan-Li/tcslib.
- Requested branch: `complexity/arora-barak-ch3-4`.
- Base: `5eb189196b117883a1db198cba87ba35db8e315b`.
- Required ancestor: `c1f9181cea266340e0e699a08d57ef414fd71a1c`; the `git merge-base --is-ancestor` check passed before work.
- Local working branch: `refactor/ch34-t1-codeformat`. No rebase, push, or PR.
- Binding sources read: `briefs/ch34-t1-item8.md`, all three archived Item8 pre-ship probes, `policy.md`, `workflow.md` §4, `AGENTS.md`, the prescribed order list, the C1 bootstrap loop and the copy-text screen.
- Six owned source files only; no blueprint or consumer source edits.

## Changes

Task 1 factors transition records, table formats, the 27-record two-work-tape table, and the one-tape instance into `CodeFormat.lean`. The seven public moves preserve their docstrings and signatures. The five one-tape wrappers use the prescribed term-mode transports. MathlibBridge exposes the generic primitive-recursion layer and canonizer construction; its existing public compiler and effective-scheme theorem remain byte-identical. NDCodes only gains the sanctioned import, loses the two verbatim moved definitions, and gains its prefix-preserving module appendix.

Task 2 replaces Codes2Tape's parser/canonizer copies by its private `twoTapeFormat` instance, retains the typed `zfBDecode` alias, and transports the size and guard facts. Only the designated malformed-code hypothesis changes. The two-tape effective-scheme theorem and conditional uniform-simulator assembly use the shared canonizer. The remaining ZF-B3 declarations are byte-identical.

### Two integration details

1. **Declaration placement.** The primitive-recursive layer and both table-skipper proofs are inserted after `codePrimPrefix`, before `BridgeAlphabet`. The new `CodeFormat.exists_canonizer` is placed immediately after `codePrim_machine`, before `codeCanonical_machine`: its proof requires that compiler, which is declared later than the brief's suggested insertion point. No existing declaration was moved, and every new signature matches the main probe. This is the only placement deviation.
2. **Axiom-preserving proof adjustment.** The archived `codeActionK_length` proof's unrestricted `simp` for the optional-state length introduced `Classical.choice` via generic ordered-addition instances. Consequently the three required public definition wrappers acquired that axiom through the `oneTapeFormat` proof fields. Replacing that one simplifier step by explicit list-length unfolding and `omega` removes the extra dependency. This changes only a new proof body, leaves its probed statement unchanged, and preserves the old public axiom sets. Both the initial diagnostic and the corrected diagnostic are included. No declaration or hypothesis was weakened.

The new CodeFormat file is 575 lines. Each new public declaration has `[AB09, §1.4]` attribution; the prescribed proof sketches are included. Existing moved docstrings are preserved exactly, including those predating the attribution convention. No new `@[simp]` lemma is introduced.

All counts meet rule 6 exactly. The 123 public declarations are the 79 baseline publics plus 44 additions. The source-union freeze log enumerates every retained declaration, sanctioned body change, move, deletion, and new declaration; new signatures are compared byte-for-byte with the main probe. The CodeFormat structure fields and both instance fields retain the probed contracts.

The only import changes are the four sanctioned ones: CodeFormat imports only CodeParser; MathlibBridge replaces CodeParser by CodeFormat; NDCodes adds CodeFormat; the facade adds CodeFormat and one Contents bullet. Codes2Tape's imports are unchanged.

The effective two-tape scheme uses the ordinary nested structure constructors after obtaining its shared canonizer. This avoids repeating the frozen one-tape scheme's named-field literal, which otherwise creates an unlisted new 50% cross-file screen pair. It does not introduce a helper or copy a proof.

## Duplication

Final screen totals: 117 → 77 directed pairs; 29,824 → 12,563 reproduced characters. All 44 directed CodeParser/Codes2Tape pairs and all seven Codes2Tape/MathlibBridge pairs disappear. Both full outputs and an itemized classification of every new directed pair are supplied.

The additional in-file similarities beyond the brief's list are the two directions of `codePrimSkipTable1`/`codePrimSkipTable2` (67%, 54%; nested applications of the shared `codePrimRepeat` theorem) and `zfB3_decode_length` → `zfB3_decode_states` (50%; generic corollary wrappers and the fixed fallback-length rewrite). They are below 90%; no new unlisted cross-file pair remains.

Codes2Tape adaptations: **23 → 1** (`zfBFallback`, the retained one-line literal). **New copies: none.**

The BridgeExport program fails at its `run_cmd` (line 59), after finding
`ROOTS Complexity.TMSAT_mem_NP: []`, because its assertion expects
`[Complexity.TMSAT_mem_NP]`. Its printed TMSAT theorem axioms already exclude
`sorryAx` at the base. The program stops at that obsolete assertion; its remaining
assertions are not claimed to pass. Both audit-program sources are untouched.
`evidence/audit-program-comparison.log` records the byte-identical outputs and
exit statuses. Five extra upstream modules were checked solely to load these
programs; their log and order are included.

The old public docstring ending immediately before the new Item-8 paragraph
contains one space before its closing delimiter. That space is deliberately
retained at the new paragraph boundary to preserve the exact prefix; it is the
single `git diff --check` trailing-space diagnostic. There is no code whitespace
error. `evidence/contracts.log` validates this exact exception.

Local commits:

- Task 1: `88a1010c` — shared record/format layer and one-tape refactor.
- Task 2: `13ab0f3f` — two-tape instance and transported consumers.

Every Lean check uses the repository's `scripts/lean_check_tree.sh`; `lake build` was never run. `lake exe cache get` completed successfully at the pinned mathlib revision. Initial environment failures and diagnostic attempts are retained separately from successful sweep logs. Lean's executable-location lookup needed a small `/proc/<pid>/exe` to `/proc/self/exe` compatibility shim because this container exposes different PID namespaces; its source is included. It changes no Lean/kernel code, axioms, compiler options or check script.

The full 244-module baseline bootstrap uses the issued order. The baseline compiled tree was snapshotted before the refactor for the legacy-program comparison; the five extra audit dependencies were added from their checked, unchanged base sources. The final replay order lists the specified 23 modules, including the direct Codes2Tape check; the root `TCSlib` is excluded. Sorry warnings are compared by module and declaration, rather than line number, because the sanctioned moves and deletions change source coordinates.

The archive contains this report, the six complete source files, the local commit patch series and bundle against the recorded base, sweep/axiom/freeze/copy-screen/lint evidence and verification programs, and `SHA256SUMS`. Verification scripts and logs do not belong to the six-file repository patch.

## ===== audits/evidence/12.2c-t1-codeformat.patch =====

From 603b3a35e36ede4bb8af7536feee384d6d128127 Mon Sep 17 00:00:00 2001
From: Codex <codex@openai.com>
Date: Sun, 11 Oct 2026 02:05:32 +0200
Subject: [PATCH 1/2] refactor: share machine-code record and format layers

---
 TCSlib/Complexity/TuringMachine.lean          |   3 +
 .../Complexity/TuringMachine/CodeFormat.lean  | 575 ++++++++++++++++++
 .../Complexity/TuringMachine/CodeParser.lean  | 354 +----------
 .../TuringMachine/MathlibBridge.lean          | 127 ++--
 TCSlib/Complexity/TuringMachine/NDCodes.lean  |  20 +-
 5 files changed, 674 insertions(+), 405 deletions(-)
 create mode 100644 TCSlib/Complexity/TuringMachine/CodeFormat.lean

diff --git a/TCSlib/Complexity/TuringMachine.lean b/TCSlib/Complexity/TuringMachine.lean
index 388012c1..b725ed0f 100644
--- a/TCSlib/Complexity/TuringMachine.lean
+++ b/TCSlib/Complexity/TuringMachine.lean
@@ -33,6 +33,7 @@ import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.Robustness.ObliviousLedger
 import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.Robustness.Oblivious
 import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.Encoding
 import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.CodeParser
+import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.CodeFormat
 import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.MathlibBridge
 import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.UniversalStartup
 import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.UniversalInterpreter
@@ -59,6 +60,8 @@ for the local modifications.
 
 ## Contents
 
+* `CodeFormat` — shared transition-record and table-format parsers, with the one-tape instance.
+
 * `Configuration` — configurations `Cfg`, actions `Action` and their application; the
   space measure. Nothing here mentions a machine (vendored).
 * `Deterministic` — `MultiTapeTM`, the step/run semantics, time and space bounds
diff --git a/TCSlib/Complexity/TuringMachine/CodeFormat.lean b/TCSlib/Complexity/TuringMachine/CodeFormat.lean
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..4c8d6da3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/TCSlib/Complexity/TuringMachine/CodeFormat.lean
@@ -0,0 +1,575 @@
+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 Seyoon Ragavan. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+Authors: Seyoon Ragavan
+-/
+import TCSlib.Complexity.TuringMachine.CodeParser
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Machine-code formats
+
+The shared record and table parser layer for fixed machine serializations.
+This factors the one- and two-work-tape constructions of [AB09, §1.4].
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `Turing.CodeFormat` — a table grammar, its laws and its fallback.
+* `Turing.actionBitsK` / `Turing.codeReadActionK` — records for any work-tape count.
+* `Turing.CodeTable2` — the shared 27-record two-work-tape table.
+* `Turing.oneTapeFormat` — the one-work-tape instance and public decoder wrappers.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `Turing.CodeFormat.decode_serialize_pad` — padded decoding recovers the machine.
+* `Turing.CodeFormat.canonical_eq` — scanning computes its canonical serialization.
+* `Turing.CodeFormat.numStates_decode_le` — the guarded decoded-size bound.
+
+## References
+
+* [AB09] S. Arora, B. Barak, *Computational Complexity: A Modern Approach*,
+  Cambridge University Press, 2009. (§1.4, pp. 19-20.)
+-/
+
+namespace Turing
+
+
+/-! ## Transition records -/
+
+/-- The serialization of a transition record lists the input move, each work-tape write
+and move in tape order, the output, and the successor state. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def actionBitsK {k n : ℕ} (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1))) : List Bool :=
+  signBits a.inputTape ++
+    (List.finRange k).flatMap
+      (fun i => optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes i).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes i).2) ++
+    optBoolBits a.output ++ optStateBits a.state
+
+/-- Read an optional work-tape write and its head move. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def codeReadWork (xs : List Bool) :
+    Option ((Option (Option Bool) × SignType) × List Bool) := do
+  let (wr, xs) ← codeReadWrite xs
+  let (wm, xs) ← codeReadSign xs
+  pure ((wr, wm), xs)
+
+/-- Read a transition record with a vector of `k` work-tape actions. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def codeReadActionK (k n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
+    Option (Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1)) × List Bool) := do
+  let (im, xs) ← codeReadSign xs
+  let (ws, xs) ← codeReadVec codeReadWork k xs
+  let (out, xs) ← codeReadOutput xs
+  let (q, xs) ← codeReadState (n + 1) xs
+  pure (⟨im, ws, out, q⟩, xs)
+
+/-- Skip a work-tape write and move, enforcing their field grammar. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def codeSkipWork (xs : List Bool) : Option (List Bool) :=
+  (codeSkipPair (fun _ _ => true) xs).bind (codeSkipPair (fun a b => a || !b))
+
+/-- Skip a transition record for `k` work tapes, checking the successor state bound. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def codeSkipActionK (k n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) : Option (List Bool) := do
+  let xs ← codeSkipPair (fun a b => a || !b) xs
+  let xs ← codeSkipRepeat codeSkipWork k xs
+  let xs ← codeSkipPair (fun a b => a || !b) xs
+  codeSkipState (n + 1) xs
+
+/-- Reading a serialized work-tape action recovers it and leaves the suffix untouched. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codeReadWork_append (p : Option (Option Bool) × SignType) (xs : List Bool) :
+    codeReadWork ((optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2) ++ xs) = some (p, xs) := by
+  rcases p with ⟨wr, wm⟩
+  simp only [codeReadWork, List.append_assoc, codeReadWrite_append, codeReadSign_append,
+    bind, Option.bind, pure]
+
+/-- Reading a serialized transition recovers it and leaves the suffix untouched. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codeReadActionK_append {k n : ℕ} (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1))) (xs : List Bool) :
+    codeReadActionK k n (actionBitsK a ++ xs) = some (a, xs) := by
+  rcases a with ⟨im, ws, out, q⟩
+  have hv : ∀ ys, codeReadVec codeReadWork k
+      ((List.finRange k).flatMap (fun i => optOptBoolBits (ws i).1 ++ signBits (ws i).2) ++ ys) =
+        some (ws, ys) :=
+    codeReadVec_append codeReadWork (fun p => optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2)
+      codeReadWork_append k ws
+  simp only [actionBitsK, List.append_assoc, codeReadActionK, codeReadSign_append, hv,
+    codeReadOutput_append, codeReadState_append, bind, Option.bind, pure]
+
+/-- A successful work-tape read consumes exactly the serialization of its result. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codeReadWork_sound (xs : List Bool) (p : Option (Option Bool) × SignType)
+    (rest : List Bool) (h : codeReadWork xs = some (p, rest)) :
+    xs = (optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2) ++ rest := by
+  simp only [codeReadWork, bind, Option.bind_eq_some_iff] at h
+  obtain ⟨⟨wr, r₁⟩, h₁, ⟨⟨wm, r₂⟩, h₂, h⟩⟩ := h
+  simp only [pure, Option.some.injEq, Prod.mk.injEq] at h
+  rcases h with ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
+  rw [codeReadWrite_sound xs wr r₁ h₁, codeReadSign_sound r₁ wm r₂ h₂]
+  simp [List.append_assoc]
+
+/-- A successful transition read consumes exactly the serialization of its result. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codeReadActionK_sound {k n : ℕ} (xs : List Bool) (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1)))
+    (rest : List Bool) (h : codeReadActionK k n xs = some (a, rest)) :
+    xs = actionBitsK a ++ rest := by
+  simp only [codeReadActionK, bind, Option.bind_eq_some_iff] at h
+  obtain ⟨⟨im, r₁⟩, h₁, ⟨⟨ws, r₂⟩, h₂, ⟨⟨out, r₃⟩, h₃, ⟨⟨q, r₄⟩, h₄, h⟩⟩⟩⟩ := h
+  simp only [pure, Option.some.injEq, Prod.mk.injEq] at h
+  rcases h with ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
+  rw [codeReadSign_sound xs im r₁ h₁,
+    codeReadVec_sound codeReadWork (fun p => optOptBoolBits p.1 ++ signBits p.2)
+      codeReadWork_sound k r₁ ws r₂ h₂,
+    codeReadOutput_sound r₂ out r₃ h₃, codeReadState_sound r₃ q r₄ h₄]
+  simp [actionBitsK, List.append_assoc]
+
+/-- Erasing the work-tape reader result gives the work-tape skipper. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codeEraseWork (xs : List Bool) : (codeReadWork xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipWork xs := by
+  simp only [codeReadWork, codeSkipWork, bind, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def, pure,
+    Option.map_some]
+  rw [← codeEraseWrite xs, ← codeErase_bind]
+  congr 1; funext p
+  simpa only [Option.map_eq_bind, Function.comp_def] using codeEraseSign p.2
+
+/-- Erasing the transition reader result gives the transition skipper. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codeEraseActionK (k n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
+    (codeReadActionK k n xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipActionK k n xs := by
+  have hv (ys : List Bool) :
+      (codeReadVec codeReadWork k ys).map Prod.snd = codeSkipRepeat codeSkipWork k ys := by
+    rw [codeEraseVec]
+    exact congrArg (fun r => codeSkipRepeat r k ys) (funext codeEraseWork)
+  simp only [codeReadActionK, codeSkipActionK, bind, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def, pure,
+    Option.map_some]
+  rw [← codeEraseSign xs, ← codeErase_bind]
+  congr 1; funext p
+  rw [← hv p.2, ← codeErase_bind]
+  congr 1; funext p
+  rw [← codeEraseOutput p.2, ← codeErase_bind]
+  congr 1; funext p
+  simpa only [Option.map_eq_bind, Function.comp_def] using codeEraseState (n + 1) p.2
+
+/-- Every `k`-work-tape record has at least `4 * k + 5` bits. [AB09, §1.4]
+
+**Proof sketch.** Each work tape contributes four bits. The input move and output contribute
+four more, and the successor-state field contributes at least one. -/
+lemma codeActionK_length {k n : ℕ} (a : Action k Bool (Fin (n + 1))) :
+    4 * k + 5 ≤ (actionBitsK a).length := by
+  have hs (s : SignType) : (signBits s).length = 2 := by cases s <;> rfl
+  have ho (b : Option Bool) : (optBoolBits b).length = 2 := by
+    rcases b with _ | b
+    · rfl
+    · cases b <;> rfl
+  have hw (b : Option (Option Bool)) : (optOptBoolBits b).length = 2 := by
+    rcases b with _ | (_ | b)
+    · rfl
+    · rfl
+    · cases b <;> rfl
+  have hq : 1 ≤ (optStateBits a.state).length := by
+    cases a.state <;>
+      simp only [optStateBits, unaryFin, List.length_cons, List.length_append,
+        List.length_replicate, List.length_nil] <;> omega
+  have hv : 4 * k ≤ ((List.finRange k).flatMap fun i =>
+      optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes i).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes i).2).length := by
+    simpa using codeFlatMap_length (List.finRange k)
+      (fun i => optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes i).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes i).2) 4
+      (fun i _ => by simp [hs, hw])
+  simp only [actionBitsK, List.length_append, hs, ho]
+  omega
+
+/-- The work-symbol function reading `w₀` on tape `0` and `w₁` on tape `1`,
+for the fixed table enumeration of `Turing.CodeNDTM.serialize`. -/
+def workPair (w₀ w₁ : Option Bool) : Fin 2 → Option Bool :=
+  fun j => if j = 0 then w₀ else w₁
+
+/-- Serialization of one two-work-tape transition record: the input-head move,
+then each work tape's optional write and move in tape order, then the emission
+and the successor state — `Turing.actionBits` with a second work-tape record. -/
+def actionBits₂ {n : ℕ} (a : Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) : List Bool :=
+  signBits a.inputTape ++
+    optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes 0).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes 0).2 ++
+    optOptBoolBits (a.workTapes 1).1 ++ signBits (a.workTapes 1).2 ++
+    optBoolBits a.output ++ optStateBits a.state
+
+/-- The fixed one-work-tape serialization agrees with the generic record serialization. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma actionBits_eq_actionBitsK {n : ℕ} (a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) :
+    actionBits a = actionBitsK a := by
+  simp [actionBits, actionBitsK, List.finRange_succ, List.append_assoc]
+
+/-- The fixed two-work-tape serialization agrees with the generic record serialization. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK {n : ℕ} (a : Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) :
+    actionBits₂ a = actionBitsK a := by
+  simp [actionBits₂, actionBitsK, List.finRange_succ, List.append_assoc]
+
+/-- The fixed one-work-tape skipper agrees with the generic skipper at `k = 1`. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codeSkipAction_eq_codeSkipActionK (n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
+    codeSkipAction n xs = codeSkipActionK 1 n xs := by
+  have h1 (ys : List Bool) : codeSkipRepeat codeSkipWork 1 ys = codeSkipWork ys := by
+    cases h : codeSkipWork ys <;> simp [codeSkipRepeat, h]
+  simp only [codeSkipAction, codeSkipActionK, h1, codeSkipWork, bind, Option.bind_assoc]
+
+/-! ## Table formats -/
+
+/-- A machine-code format specifies a transition-table grammar under the shared
+state-count and initial-state header, together with its parser laws and fallback. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+structure CodeFormat where
+  /-- the coded machines -/
+  Code : Type
+  /-- the transition table of a machine with `n + 1` states -/
+  Table : ℕ → Type
+  /-- one less than the number of states -/
+  numStates : Code → ℕ
+  /-- assemble a machine from its state count, initial state and table -/
+  assemble : (n : ℕ) → Fin (n + 1) → Table n → Code
+  /-- the format's fixed serialization -/
+  serialize : Code → List Bool
+  /-- the fixed serialization of a table -/
+  tableBits : {n : ℕ} → Table n → List Bool
+  /-- the table reader -/
+  readTable : (n : ℕ) → List Bool → Option (Table n × List Bool)
+  /-- the erased table reader -/
+  skipTable : ℕ → List Bool → Option (List Bool)
+  /-- the minimum number of table bits per state (the up-front guard constant) -/
+  guard : ℕ
+  /-- the machine denoted by every malformed string -/
+  fallback : Code
+  numStates_assemble : ∀ n q t, numStates (assemble n q t) = n
+  serialize_assemble : ∀ n q (t : Table n),
+    serialize (assemble n q t) = pairEncode n.bits (unaryFin q ++ tableBits t)
+  assemble_surj : ∀ M, ∃ n q t, assemble n q t = M
+  readTable_append : ∀ n (t : Table n) xs, readTable n (tableBits t ++ xs) = some (t, xs)
+  readTable_sound : ∀ n xs (t : Table n) rest,
+    readTable n xs = some (t, rest) → xs = tableBits t ++ rest
+  readTable_erase : ∀ n xs, (readTable n xs).map Prod.snd = skipTable n xs
+  tableBits_length : ∀ n (t : Table n), guard * (n + 1) ≤ (tableBits t).length
+
+namespace CodeFormat
+
+variable (F : CodeFormat)
+
+/-- Parse a machine and retain its all-true padding; check the table-length guard
+before reading any state-indexed table entries. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def parseFull (xs : List Bool) : Option (F.Code × List Bool) := do
+  let (bits, rest) ← pairDecode xs
+  let n := codeBitsNat bits
+  if bits ≠ n.bits then none else do
+    if F.guard * (n + 1) > rest.length then none else do
+      let (q, rest) ← codeReadFin (n + 1) rest
+      let (table, rest) ← F.readTable n rest
+      if rest.all id then pure (F.assemble n q table, rest) else none
+
+/-- Decode every string, choosing the format's fallback on any malformed input. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def decode (xs : List Bool) : F.Code := ((F.parseFull xs).map Prod.fst).getD F.fallback
+
+/-- Check the same header, table-length guard and padding as the parser,
+retaining only the unconsumed suffix. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def scan (xs : List Bool) : Option (List Bool) := do
+  let (bits, rest) ← pairDecode xs
+  let n := codeBitsNat bits
+  if bits ≠ n.bits then none else do
+    if F.guard * (n + 1) > rest.length then none else do
+      let rest ← codeSkipFin (n + 1) rest
+      let rest ← F.skipTable n rest
+      if rest.all id then pure rest else none
+
+/-- Return the consumed prefix when scanning succeeds and the fallback's serialization
+when scanning fails. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def canonical (xs : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  (F.scan xs).casesOn (F.serialize F.fallback) fun tail => xs.take (xs.length - tail.length)
+
+/-- Parsing a serialization followed by true padding recovers the machine and padding. [AB09, §1.4]
+
+**Proof sketch.** The doubled header recovers the canonical state-count bits. The table-length
+bound discharges the early guard; the initial-state and table inverse laws recover
+the assembled machine and leave the replicated true suffix, which passes the padding test. -/
+theorem parseFull_serialize_pad (M : F.Code) (m : ℕ) :
+    F.parseFull (F.serialize M ++ List.replicate m true) =
+      some (M, List.replicate m true) := by
+  obtain ⟨n, q, t, rfl⟩ := F.assemble_surj M
+  have hp (a b c : List Bool) : pairEncode a b ++ c = pairEncode a (b ++ c) := by
+    simp [pairEncode, List.append_assoc]
+  rw [F.serialize_assemble, hp]
+  unfold parseFull
+  rw [pairDecode_pairEncode]
+  dsimp only [bind, Option.bind]
+  rw [codeBitsNat_bits]
+  simp only [ne_eq, not_true_eq_false, ↓reduceIte]
+  have hlen := F.tableBits_length n t
+  simp only [List.length_append, List.length_replicate] at *
+  rw [if_neg (by omega)]
+  simp only [List.append_assoc, codeReadFin_append, F.readTable_append,
+    List.all_replicate, id_eq, ite_self, ↓reduceIte, pure]
+
+/-- Decoding a serialization followed by arbitrary true padding recovers the machine. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+theorem decode_serialize_pad (M : F.Code) (m : ℕ) :
+    F.decode (F.serialize M ++ List.replicate m true) = M := by
+  simp only [decode, parseFull_serialize_pad, Option.map_some, Option.getD_some]
+
+/-- Every successful parse expresses the input as the decoded serialization and suffix. [AB09, §1.4]
+
+**Proof sketch.** Decompose the successful parse and use the field and table soundness equations.
+The canonical state-count check reconstructs the doubled header, giving exactly
+the machine serialization followed by the retained suffix. -/
+theorem parseFull_sound (xs : List Bool) (M : F.Code) (tail : List Bool)
+    (h : F.parseFull xs = some (M, tail)) : xs = F.serialize M ++ tail := by
+  unfold parseFull at h
+  obtain ⟨⟨bits, rest⟩, hp, h⟩ := Option.bind_eq_some_iff.mp h
+  dsimp only at h
+  split at h
+  · contradiction
+  next hb =>
+    have hb : bits = (codeBitsNat bits).bits := not_not.mp hb
+    split at h
+    · contradiction
+    next _ =>
+      simp only [bind, Option.bind_eq_some_iff] at h
+      obtain ⟨⟨q, r₁⟩, hq, ⟨⟨table, r₂⟩, ht, h⟩⟩ := h
+      split at h
+      next hpad =>
+        simp only [pure, Option.some.injEq, Prod.mk.injEq] at h
+        rcases h with ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
+        have htable := F.readTable_sound _ _ _ _ ht
+        dsimp only at htable
+        rw [F.serialize_assemble, eq_pairEncode_of_pairDecode xs bits rest hp,
+          codeReadFin_sound rest q r₁ hq, htable]
+        simp only [pairEncode, List.append_assoc]
+        congr 1
+        exact congrArg (List.flatMap fun b : Bool => [b, b]) hb
+      · contradiction
+
+/-- The erased scanner returns exactly the suffix retained by the full parser. [AB09, §1.4]
+
+**Proof sketch.** Push suffix projection through the parser's binds and branches. The initial-state
+and table erasure laws replace their readers by the corresponding skippers; the
+header checks and final padding test are unchanged. -/
+theorem scan_full (xs : List Bool) : F.scan xs = (F.parseFull xs).map Prod.snd := by
+  simp only [scan, parseFull, bind, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def]
+  congr 1
+  funext p
+  dsimp only
+  split <;> try simp only [Option.map_none]
+  split <;> try simp only [Option.map_none, Option.map_bind, Function.comp_def]
+  have h (q : Fin (codeBitsNat p.1 + 1) × List Bool) (t : F.Table (codeBitsNat p.1) × List Bool) :
+      (if t.2.all id then some (F.assemble (codeBitsNat p.1) q.1 t.1, t.2) else none).map
+          Prod.snd = (if t.2.all id then some t.2 else none) := by split <;> rfl
+  simp only [pure, h]
+  rw [← codeEraseFin _ _, ← codeErase_bind]
+  congr 1; funext q
+  rw [← F.readTable_erase, ← codeErase_bind]
+
+/-- The scanner-based canonical prefix equals the serialization of the decoded machine. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+theorem canonical_eq (xs : List Bool) : F.canonical xs = F.serialize (F.decode xs) := by
+  unfold canonical decode
+  rw [scan_full]
+  cases h : F.parseFull xs with
+  | none => rfl
+  | some p =>
+    rcases p with ⟨M, tail⟩
+    simp only [Option.map_some, Option.getD_some]
+    rw [F.parseFull_sound xs M tail h]
+    simp
+
+/-- The decoded serialization is no longer than the input or the fixed fallback,
+whichever is longer. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+theorem serialize_decode_length_le (α : List Bool) :
+    (F.serialize (F.decode α)).length ≤ max α.length (F.serialize F.fallback).length := by
+  rw [← F.canonical_eq]
+  unfold canonical
+  cases F.scan α with
+  | none => exact Nat.le_max_right _ _
+  | some tail => exact (List.length_take_le' _ _).trans (Nat.le_max_left _ _)
+
+/-- The guarded table-size lower bound for the decoded machine is bounded by the
+input length or the fallback length, whichever is larger. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+theorem numStates_decode_le (α : List Bool) :
+    F.guard * (F.numStates (F.decode α) + 1) ≤
+      max α.length (F.serialize F.fallback).length := by
+  have hsize := F.serialize_decode_length_le α
+  obtain ⟨n, q, t, h⟩ := F.assemble_surj (F.decode α)
+  rw [← h, F.serialize_assemble, length_pairEncode, List.length_append] at hsize
+  rw [← h, F.numStates_assemble]
+  have htable := F.tableBits_length n t
+  omega
+
+/-- A failed full parse decodes to the format's fallback. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+theorem decode_of_parseFull_none (α : List Bool) (h : F.parseFull α = none) :
+    F.decode α = F.fallback := by
+  simp [decode, h]
+
+/-- A failed table-length guard decodes to the fallback before table recursion. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+theorem decode_of_guard_lt (α bits rest : List Bool) (hp : pairDecode α = some (bits, rest))
+    (hg : rest.length < F.guard * (codeBitsNat bits + 1)) : F.decode α = F.fallback := by
+  simp [decode, parseFull, hp, hg]
+
+end CodeFormat
+
+/-! ## Two-work-tape tables -/
+
+/-- A two-work-tape transition table is indexed by state, input read, and both work reads. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+abbrev CodeTable2 (n : ℕ) : Type :=
+  Fin (n + 1) → Option Bool → Option Bool → Option Bool → Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1))
+
+/-- Serialize all two-work-tape table entries in state, input, work-0, work-1 order. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def codeTable2Bits {n : ℕ} (t : CodeTable2 n) : List Bool :=
+  (List.finRange (n + 1)).flatMap fun q =>
+    ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
+      ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₀ =>
+        ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
+          actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)
+
+/-- Read the 27 two-work-tape transition records for every state. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def codeReadTable2 (n : ℕ) : List Bool → Option (CodeTable2 n × List Bool) :=
+  codeReadVec (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 2 n)))) (n + 1)
+
+/-- Skip the 27 two-work-tape transition records for every state. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+def codeSkipTable2 (n : ℕ) : List Bool → Option (List Bool) :=
+  codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipActionK 2 n) 3) 3) 3)
+    (n + 1)
+
+/-- Reading a serialized two-work-tape table recovers it and leaves the suffix untouched. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codeReadTable2_append {n : ℕ} (t : CodeTable2 n) (xs : List Bool) :
+    codeReadTable2 n (codeTable2Bits t ++ xs) = some (t, xs) :=
+  codeReadVec_append _ _ (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _
+    (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _
+      (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _ codeReadActionK_append _ _) _ _) _ _) _ _ _
+
+/-- A successful two-work-tape table read consumes exactly its serialization. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codeReadTable2_sound {n : ℕ} (xs : List Bool) (t : CodeTable2 n) (rest : List Bool)
+    (h : codeReadTable2 n xs = some (t, rest)) : xs = codeTable2Bits t ++ rest :=
+  codeReadVec_sound _ _ (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _
+    (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _
+      (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _ codeReadActionK_sound _ _ _) _ _ _) _ _ _)
+    _ _ _ _ h
+
+/-- Erasing the two-work-tape table reader result gives the corresponding skipper. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codeEraseTable2 (n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
+    (codeReadTable2 n xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipTable2 n xs := by
+  have ht := codeEraseVec
+    (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 2 n)))) (n + 1) xs
+  simp only [codeEraseSymbols, codeEraseActionK] at ht
+  exact ht
+
+/-- Every two-work-tape table has at least 351 bits per state. [AB09, §1.4]
+
+**Proof sketch.** Every record has at least 13 bits. Sum this lower bound over each of the
+three possible symbols on each of three reads, giving 27 records and 351 bits
+per state; then sum over the state enumeration. -/
+lemma codeTable2Bits_length {n : ℕ} (t : CodeTable2 n) :
+    351 * (n + 1) ≤ (codeTable2Bits t).length := by
+  have hr : ∀ a : Action 2 Bool (Fin (n + 1)), 13 ≤ (actionBitsK a).length :=
+    fun a => by have := codeActionK_length a; omega
+  have h := codeFlatMap_length (List.finRange (n + 1))
+    (fun q => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
+      ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₀ =>
+        ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
+          actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 351 (by
+      intro q _
+      have h := codeFlatMap_length ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
+        (fun inp => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₀ =>
+          ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
+            actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 117 (by
+          intro inp _
+          have h := codeFlatMap_length ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
+            (fun w₀ => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w₁ =>
+              actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 39 (by
+                intro w₀ _
+                simpa using codeFlatMap_length
+                  ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
+                  (fun w₁ => actionBitsK (t q inp w₀ w₁)) 13 (fun _ _ => hr _))
+          simpa using h)
+      simpa using h)
+  simpa [codeTable2Bits] using h
+
+/-! ## One-work-tape instance -/
+
+private lemma codeReadActionK_one_append {n : ℕ} (a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1))) (xs : List Bool) :
+    codeReadActionK 1 n (actionBits a ++ xs) = some (a, xs) := by
+  rw [actionBits_eq_actionBitsK]; exact codeReadActionK_append a xs
+
+private lemma codeReadActionK_one_sound {n : ℕ} (xs : List Bool) (a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1)))
+    (rest : List Bool) (h : codeReadActionK 1 n xs = some (a, rest)) :
+    xs = actionBits a ++ rest := by
+  rw [actionBits_eq_actionBitsK]; exact codeReadActionK_sound xs a rest h
+
+private lemma codeEraseActionK_one (n : ℕ) (xs : List Bool) :
+    (codeReadActionK 1 n xs).map Prod.snd = codeSkipAction n xs := by
+  rw [codeEraseActionK, codeSkipAction_eq_codeSkipActionK]
+
+/-- The one-work-tape `CodeTM` format has nine records per state and guard 81. [AB09, §1.4]
+
+**Proof sketch.** The frozen record serialization is the generic one at one work tape. Nested
+vector and symbol-reader laws provide table inversion, soundness and erasure;
+summing the nine-bit record bound over the nine records gives guard 81. -/
+def oneTapeFormat : CodeFormat where
+  Code := CodeTM
+  Table := fun n => Fin (n + 1) → Option Bool → Option Bool → Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1))
+  numStates := CodeTM.numStates
+  assemble := fun n q t => ⟨n, ⟨q, fun s inp w => t s inp (w 0)⟩⟩
+  serialize := CodeTM.serialize
+  tableBits := fun {n} t => (List.finRange (n + 1)).flatMap fun q =>
+    ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
+      ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w =>
+        actionBits (t q inp w)
+  readTable := fun n =>
+    codeReadVec (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 1 n))) (n + 1)
+  skipTable := fun n =>
+    codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipRepeat (codeSkipAction n) 3) 3) (n + 1)
+  guard := 81
+  fallback := codeFallback
+  numStates_assemble := fun _ _ _ => rfl
+  serialize_assemble := fun _ _ _ => rfl
+  assemble_surj := by
+    rintro ⟨n, q, tr⟩
+    refine ⟨n, q, fun s inp w => tr s inp (fun _ => w), ?_⟩
+    show CodeTM.mk n ⟨q, fun s inp w => tr s inp (fun _ => w 0)⟩ = CodeTM.mk n ⟨q, tr⟩
+    congr 1
+    congr 1
+    funext s inp w
+    apply congrArg (tr s inp)
+    funext i
+    exact congrArg w (Subsingleton.elim _ _)
+  readTable_append := fun _ _ _ =>
+    codeReadVec_append _ _ (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _
+      (fun _ _ => codeReadSymbols_append _ _ codeReadActionK_one_append _ _) _ _) _ _ _
+  readTable_sound := fun _ _ _ _ h =>
+    codeReadVec_sound _ _ (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _
+      (fun _ _ _ => codeReadSymbols_sound _ _ codeReadActionK_one_sound _ _ _) _ _ _)
+      _ _ _ _ h
+  readTable_erase := fun n xs => by
+    have ht := codeEraseVec (codeReadSymbols (codeReadSymbols (codeReadActionK 1 n))) (n + 1) xs
+    simp only [codeEraseSymbols, codeEraseActionK_one] at ht
+    exact ht
+  tableBits_length := fun n t => by
+    have hr : ∀ a : Action 1 Bool (Fin (n + 1)), 9 ≤ (actionBits a).length := fun a => by
+      rw [actionBits_eq_actionBitsK]; have := codeActionK_length a; omega
+    have h := codeFlatMap_length (List.finRange (n + 1))
+      (fun q => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun inp =>
+        ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w =>
+          actionBits (t q inp w)) 81 (by
+        intro q _
+        have h := codeFlatMap_length ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
+          (fun inp => ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool)).flatMap fun w =>
+            actionBits (t q inp w)) 27 (by
+              intro inp _
+              simpa using codeFlatMap_length
+                ([none, some false, some true] : List (Option Bool))
+                (fun w => actionBits (t q inp w)) 9 (fun _ _ => hr _))
+        simpa using h)
+    simpa using h
+
+/-- Total decoding: every malformed string denotes the fixed fallback. -/
+def codeDecode (xs : List Bool) : CodeTM :=
+  oneTapeFormat.decode xs
+
+/-- The total decoder satisfies the required exact padded round-trip law. -/
+lemma codeDecode_serialize_pad (M : CodeTM) (m : ℕ) :
+    codeDecode (M.serialize ++ List.replicate m true) = M :=
+  oneTapeFormat.decode_serialize_pad M m
+
+/-- The erased parser: accept exactly the strings the parser accepts, returning only the unconsumed all-true suffix. -/
+def codeScan (xs : List Bool) : Option (List Bool) :=
+  oneTapeFormat.scan xs
+
+/-- The canonical serialization of the machine a string denotes: the consumed prefix on scanner success, the fallback machine's serialization otherwise. -/
+def codeCanonical (xs : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  oneTapeFormat.canonical xs

+lemma codeCanonical_eq (xs : List Bool) : codeCanonical xs = (codeDecode xs).serialize :=
+  oneTapeFormat.canonical_eq xs

+end Turing

+/-- The work-tape field skipper is primitive recursive. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codePrimSkipWork : Primrec codeSkipWork :=
+  Primrec.option_bind (codePrimSkipPair _) ((codePrimSkipPair _).comp Primrec.snd).to₂
+
+/-- For each work-tape count, the transition skipper is primitive recursive
+in the state bound and input. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codePrimSkipActionK (k : ℕ) : Primrec₂ (codeSkipActionK k) := by
+  have hrep : Primrec (codeSkipRepeat codeSkipWork k) :=
+    (codePrimRepeat (fun (_ : ℕ) => codeSkipWork) (codePrimSkipWork.comp Primrec.snd).to₂
+      (fun _ => k) (Primrec.const k)).comp (Primrec.const 0) Primrec.id
+  unfold codeSkipActionK
+  apply Primrec.option_bind ((codePrimSkipPair _).comp Primrec.snd)
+  change Primrec _
+  apply Primrec.option_bind (hrep.comp Primrec.snd)
+  change Primrec _
+  apply Primrec.option_bind ((codePrimSkipPair _).comp Primrec.snd)
+  change Primrec _
+  exact codePrimSkipState.comp (Primrec.succ.comp
+    (Primrec.fst.comp (Primrec.fst.comp (Primrec.fst.comp Primrec.fst)))) Primrec.snd
+
+/-- A format's scanner is primitive recursive whenever its table skipper is. [AB09, §1.4]
+
+**Proof sketch.** Compose the primitive recursive header reader, comparisons and field skipper
+with the supplied table skipper in parser order. The final branch checks that
+the suffix consists entirely of true bits. -/
+theorem CodeFormat.primrec_scan (F : CodeFormat) (h : Primrec₂ F.skipTable) :
+    Primrec F.scan := by
+  unfold CodeFormat.scan
+  apply Primrec.option_bind codePrimPair
+  change Primrec _
+  apply Primrec.ite ((Primrec.eq.comp (Primrec.fst.comp Primrec.snd)
+    (codePrimBits.comp (codePrimBitsNat.comp (Primrec.fst.comp Primrec.snd)))).not)
+    (Primrec.const none)
+  apply Primrec.ite (Primrec.nat_lt.comp
+    (Primrec.list_length.comp (Primrec.snd.comp Primrec.snd))
+    (Primrec.nat_mul.comp (Primrec.const F.guard)
+      (Primrec.succ.comp (codePrimBitsNat.comp (Primrec.fst.comp Primrec.snd)))))
+    (Primrec.const none)
+  apply Primrec.option_bind (codePrimSkipFin.comp
+    (Primrec.succ.comp (codePrimBitsNat.comp (Primrec.fst.comp Primrec.snd)))
+    (Primrec.snd.comp Primrec.snd))
+  change Primrec _
+  apply Primrec.option_bind (h.comp
+    (codePrimBitsNat.comp (Primrec.fst.comp (Primrec.snd.comp Primrec.fst))) Primrec.snd)
+  change Primrec _
+  exact Primrec.ite (Primrec.eq.comp (codePrimAll.comp Primrec.snd) (Primrec.const true))
+    (Primrec.option_some.comp Primrec.snd) (Primrec.const none)
+
+/-- A format's canonical-prefix function is primitive recursive whenever its table skipper is. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+theorem CodeFormat.primrec_canonical (F : CodeFormat) (h : Primrec₂ F.skipTable) :

+  Primrec.option_casesOn (F.primrec_scan h) (Primrec.const (F.serialize F.fallback))

+/-- The one-work-tape table skipper is primitive recursive. -/
+private lemma codePrimSkipTable1 : Primrec₂ oneTapeFormat.skipTable := by
+  have ha : Primrec₂ codeSkipAction :=

+  have h := codePrimRepeat _ (codePrimRepeat _ (codePrimRepeat _ ha
+    (fun _ => 3) (Primrec.const 3)) (fun _ => 3) (Primrec.const 3)) (fun n => n + 1) Primrec.succ
+  exact h

+/-- The two-work-tape table skipper is primitive recursive in the state bound and input. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+lemma codePrimSkipTable2 : Primrec₂ codeSkipTable2 := by
+  have h := codePrimRepeat _ (codePrimRepeat _ (codePrimRepeat _ (codePrimRepeat _
+    (codePrimSkipActionK 2) (fun _ => 3) (Primrec.const 3)) (fun _ => 3) (Primrec.const 3))

+/-- A format with a primitive recursive table skipper has a finite binary canonizer
+with an arbitrary length-only time bound. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+theorem CodeFormat.exists_canonizer (F : CodeFormat) (h : Primrec₂ F.skipTable) :
+    ∃ (M : FinTM Bool) (T : ℕ → ℕ),
+      M.ComputesFunInTime (fun xs => F.serialize (F.decode xs)) T := by
+  obtain ⟨M, T, hM⟩ := codePrim_machine F.canonical (F.primrec_canonical h)
+  have he : F.canonical = fun xs => F.serialize (F.decode xs) := funext F.canonical_eq
+  rw [he] at hM
+  exact ⟨M, T, hM⟩
+

 its 354-bit regression; their one-tape counterparts and retained proof
 material are itemized in this delivery's duplication ledger.
+
+## Item-8 appendix
+
+The two-work-tape parser and canonizer now instantiate the shared `CodeFormat`
+layer. ZF-B3 consumes its decoded-size and guard corollaries, with the existing
+silent fallback and every public scheme statement preserved.

-unary reader, suffix-skipping primitives, pairing laws, and `codePrim_machine`
-are cited directly. No ND existence theorem is used.
-
-ZF-B2 now cites all 46 promoted format-independent declarations directly.

+this format's parser and canonizer. The common record, table and primitive-recursion
+laws live in `CodeFormat` and `MathlibBridge`; `zfBDecode` remains a typed alias
+for the frozen uniform-simulator statements.

+/-- The deterministic two-work-tape code format, with 27 records per state
+and the fixed silent fallback. [AB09, §1.4] -/
+private def twoTapeFormat : CodeFormat where
+  Code := Code2TM
+  Table := CodeTable2
+  numStates := Code2TM.numStates
+  assemble := fun n q t => ⟨n, ⟨q, fun s inp w => t s inp (w 0) (w 1)⟩⟩
+  serialize := Code2TM.serialize
+  tableBits := fun t => codeTable2Bits t
+  readTable := codeReadTable2
+  skipTable := codeSkipTable2
+  guard := 351
+  fallback := zfBFallback
+  numStates_assemble := fun _ _ _ => rfl
+  serialize_assemble := fun _ _ _ => by
+    simp [Code2TM.serialize, codeTable2Bits, actionBits₂_eq_actionBitsK, workPair]
+  assemble_surj := by
+    rintro ⟨n, q, tr⟩
+    refine ⟨n, q, fun s inp w₀ w₁ => tr s inp (workPair w₀ w₁), ?_⟩
+    show Code2TM.mk n ⟨q, fun s inp w => tr s inp (workPair (w 0) (w 1))⟩ = Code2TM.mk n ⟨q, tr⟩
     congr 1
-    cases tm with
-    | mk q tr =>
-      congr 1
-      funext s inp w
-      apply congrArg (tr s inp)
-      funext i
-      fin_cases i <;> simp [workPair]
-
